# 生产环境客服问题常用数据库表

> 来源：`生产环境客服问题常用数据库表收集.xlsx`（工作表1），共 87 条记录。
> 用途：客服排查问题时的库表速查手册。

## 一、概览

| 业务分类 | 库名 | 表数量 | 数据表 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 商创 | `lyy_consumption` | 1 | `lyy_request_history` |
| 娱乐 | `cmember` | 3 | `tiktok_activity_order`、`tiktok_activity_poi`、`tiktok_activity_coupon` |
| 娱乐 | `master` | 2 | `lyy_media_ads_device`、`MEITUAN_STORE_APPLY` |
| 娱乐 | `massage_chair` | 1 | `coins_saver_order` |
| 多金宝 | `small_venue` | 1 | `t_merchant_card` |
| 充电桩 | `master` | 5 | `merchant_equipment`、`agent_equipment`、`ad_org`、`ad_org_attach`、`equipment_charging_type` |
| 充电桩 | `massage_chair` | 5 | `lyy_charging_order`、`lyy_charging_order_power`、`lyy_charging_equipment_port`、`lyy_ic_card`、`lyy_ic_card_flow` |
| 充电桩 | `advert` | 8 | `lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card`、`lyy_cdz_insurance_order`、`lyy_cdz_activity_black_list`、`cdz_debit_order`、`automatic_debit_order`、`lyy_cdz_member_card`、`lyy_cdz_insurance_equipment_order_detail`、`lyy_distributor_advertise_coupon` |
| 充电桩 | `commercial_charges` | 4 | `system_charge_equipment_bill`、`system_charge_equipment_bill_flow`、`system_charge_equipment`、`system_charge_config` |
| 汽充桩 | `carcharging` | 4 | `infra_station`、`order_charge`、`family_equipment_vip`、`member_user_info` |
| 中台 | `commercial_charges` | 8 | `merchant_product`、`receipt_bill`、`receipt_bill_detail`、`receipt_bill_pay_trans`、`receipt_bill_pay_trans_detail`、`receipt_bill_refund_trans`、`receipt_bill_refund_trans_detail`、`merchant_message_config` |
| 中台 | `user_member` | 5 | `um_account_record`、`um_account`、`um_account_benefit`、`um_benefit_scope`、`um_benefit` |
| 中台 | `selectdb_payment` | 5 | `dwd_payment_payment_record_trade`、`dwd_payment_refund_record_trade`、`ads_order_info_agg`、`ods_unified_redeem_order`、`ads_payment_equipment_record_di` |
| 洗水 | `cmember` | 3 | `wash_dimension_user_info`、`promotion_activity_merchant`、`wash_product_order` |
| 洗水 | `merchant` | 1 | `merchant_equipment_board` |
| 洗水 | `middle_stage` | 1 | `ticket` |
| 洗水 | `massage_chair` | 3 | `lyy_ic_card`、`lyy_ic_card_flow`、`ic_card_limit_config` |
| 洗水 | `lyy_infor` | 5 | `xiaole_user_points_record`、`xiaole_life_user`、`xiaole_life_user_relate`、`xiaole_black_user`、`xiaole_user_no_app_request` |
| 洗水 | `order` | 2 | `order_info`、`refund_order_info` |
| 设备 | `equipment` | 4 | `device`、`iot_equipment`、`iot_equipment_setting`、`iot_equipment_docking` |
| 设备 | `master` | 1 | `lyy_equipment_private_key` |
| 支付 | `master` | 7 | `payment_fee_deduction_equipment`、`experience_merchant_list`、`lyy_merchant_info`、`lyy_divide_merchant_info`、`lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail`、`lyy_payment`、`payment_info_config` |
| 按摩 | `advert` | 4 | `insurance_user_benefit_card`、`insurance_order`、`automatic_debit_order`、`automatic_debit_receive_record` |
| 按摩 | `cmember` | 3 | `massage_automatic_debit`、`jd_insurance_start_record`、`promotion_activity_user` |

合计：**10** 个业务分类、**15** 个库、**87** 条表记录（去重后 **84** 张表）。

## 二、库与表明细

### 商创

**库：`lyy_consumption`**（1 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `lyy_request_history` | 查询商家风控补贴明细 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE lyy_request_history (
    lyy_request_history_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(11,0),
    lyy_fans_id numeric(12,0),
    lyy_task_id numeric(11,0),
    amount numeric,
    distributorpart numeric,
    shopkeeperpart numeric,
    price numeric(8,2),
    code character varying(32),
    ad_org_id numeric(10,0),
    ad_client_id numeric(10,0),
    isactive character(1),
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    createdby numeric(10,0),
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updatedby numeric(10,0),
    yxb_incid character varying(16) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    lyy_trade_transaction_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    agent_price numeric(8,2),
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    factorypart numeric(6,2),
    isvalid character(1) DEFAULT 'Y'::bpchar,
    lyy_factory_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_online_coins_history_id numeric(12,0),
    is_statistics character(1),
    out_trade_no character varying(32),
    agentpart numeric(6,2),
    is_agent_statistics character(1),
    agent_user_id numeric(10,0),
    online_subsidy boolean,
    card_id bigint,
    settle_type smallint,
    main_distributor bigint,
    store_id numeric(12,0),
    wallet_user_id numeric(12,0),
    settlement_way character varying(20),
    receive_pay_account character varying(30)
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (created);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.is_statistics is '是否已算入工厂收益（Y/N）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.out_trade_no is '广告收益所属订单';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.online_subsidy is '是否在线补贴';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.card_id is '资料ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.settle_type is '结算方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.main_distributor is '主营商家';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.store_id is '多金宝门店id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.wallet_user_id is '多金宝钱包id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.settlement_way is '清分结算方式，wallet,channel';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_request_history.receive_pay_account is '清分商户号';
```

```sql
-- 【lyy_request_history】查询商家风控补贴明细
SELECT * FROM lyy_request_history WHERE ad_org_id = 1497193 AND created  >= '2024-01-01 00:00:00'
```

### 娱乐

**库：`cmember`**（3 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `tiktok_activity_order` | 查询抖音订单 |
| `tiktok_activity_poi` | 查询抖音商户门店 |
| `tiktok_activity_coupon` | 查询抖音商户授权 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE lyy.tiktok_activity_order (
    tiktok_activity_order_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_equipment_type_id numeric(10,0),
    equipment_value character varying(40),
    tiktok_code character varying(40),
    title character varying(60),
    status smallint,
    details jsonb,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    poi_id character varying(40),
    redeem_type smallint,
    sku_id character varying(40),
    lyy_user_id bigint
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (created);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.tiktok_activity_order_id is '抖音POI活动订单ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.lyy_distributor_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.lyy_equipment_group_id is '场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.lyy_equipment_type_id is '设备类型ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.equipment_value is '设备value';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.tiktok_code is '券码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.title is '团单名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.status is '状态 1 初始化 2 成功 3 失败';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.details is '详情';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.poi_id is '商家poi_id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.redeem_type is '核销类型 null: 反扫核销; 2: 抖音套餐核销;';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.sku_id is '商品sku_id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.tiktok_activity_order.lyy_user_id is '乐摇摇用户ID';

CREATE TABLE tiktok_activity_poi (
    tiktok_activity_poi_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_distributor_name character varying(40),
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0),
    poi_id character varying(40),
    poi_name character varying(40),
    address character varying(200),
    latitude numeric(12,8),
    longitude numeric(12,8),
    show_switch smallint DEFAULT 1,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    createdby numeric(10,0),
    updatedby numeric(10,0),
    isactive character(1) DEFAULT 'Y'::bpchar,
    account_id character varying(40)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.tiktok_activity_poi_id is '抖音活动POI';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.lyy_distributor_name is '商家名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.lyy_distributor_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.poi_id is '抖音poi';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.poi_name is '店名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.address is '地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.latitude is '纬度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.longitude is '经度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.show_switch is '展示开关 0 关闭 1 开启';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.createdby is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.updatedby is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.isactive is '是否有效 Y 有效 N 无效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_poi.account_id is '商家抖音账号id';
COMMENT ON TABLE tiktok_activity_poi is '抖音POI活动';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX tiktok_activity_poi_pkey ON tiktok_activity_poi USING btree (tiktok_activity_poi_id);
CREATE INDEX tiktok_activity_poi_lyy_distributor_id_account_id_index ON tiktok_activity_poi USING btree (lyy_distributor_id, account_id);

CREATE TABLE tiktok_activity_coupon (
    tiktok_activity_coupon_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    tiktok_activity_poi_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    poi_id character varying(40),
    sku_id character varying(40),
    title character varying(40),
    price numeric(10,2),
    coins integer DEFAULT 0,
    status smallint DEFAULT 0,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    createdby numeric(10,0),
    updatedby numeric(10,0),
    isactive character(1) DEFAULT 'Y'::bpchar,
    use_type smallint,
    auth_created_by character varying(20),
    auth_updated_by character varying(20),
    order_no smallint,
    display_on_coin_exchange boolean,
    equipment_type_value character varying[],
    redeem_mode smallint,
    recharge_type smallint,
    expire_date jsonb,
    mock_coins integer
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.tiktok_activity_coupon_id is '抖音套餐映射Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.tiktok_activity_poi_id is '抖音活动Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.poi_id is '门店poi_id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.sku_id is '抖音sku_id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.title is '套餐名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.price is '价格';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.coins is '获取权益,use_type=null或1时表示(币数),  user_type=2时表示需要给用户派发商家派发金额(分)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.status is 'poi状态,默认 0:投放中, 1:已终止';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.createdby is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.updatedby is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.isactive is '是否有效 Y 有效 N 无效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.use_type is '适用类型: null或1表示娃娃机与兑币机适用  2表示洗车机';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.auth_created_by is 'createdby=-10000时展示,sp操作人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.auth_updated_by is 'updatedby=-10000时展示,sp操作人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.order_no is '排序编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.display_on_coin_exchange is '在兑币机上展示';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.equipment_type_value is '设备类型，use_type=4时使用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.redeem_mode is '核销方式：1直接使用/启动设备，2充值到会员账户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.recharge_type is '充值类型：1余额，2余币，仅redeem_mode=2时生效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.expire_date is '有效期配置：category为0或null表示无期限，1表示自定义';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tiktok_activity_coupon.mock_coins is '模拟投币数';
COMMENT ON TABLE tiktok_activity_coupon is '抖音套餐映射';
CREATE INDEX tiktok_activity_coupon_sku_activity_created_idx ON tiktok_activity_coupon USING btree (sku_id, tiktok_activity_poi_id, created);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX tiktok_activity_coupon_pkey ON tiktok_activity_coupon USING btree (tiktok_activity_coupon_id);
```

```sql
-- 【tiktok_activity_order】查询抖音订单
select * from tiktok_activity_order where sku_id='7284593202361796669'

-- 【tiktok_activity_poi】查询抖音商户门店
select * from tiktok_activity_poi where poi_id='7284593202361796669'

-- 【tiktok_activity_coupon】查询抖音商户授权
select * from tiktok_activity_coupon where poi_id='7284593202361796669'  and isactive='Y' and sku_id='1838044671248384'
```

**库：`master`**（2 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `lyy_media_ads_device` | 查询兑币机下发广告记录 |
| `MEITUAN_STORE_APPLY` | 查询美团授权记录 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device (
    lyy_media_ads_device_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_media_ads_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    status numeric(2,0),
    lyy_mainboard_position_id numeric(10,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device.lyy_media_ads_device_id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device.lyy_media_ads_id is '对应的媒体广告ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device.lyy_equipment_id is '对应的设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device.status is '3-待播放,4·播放中,5-已停止,6-已移除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device.lyy_mainboard_position_id is '仓位id';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device is '媒体广告投放设备表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_lyy_media_ads_device ON adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device USING btree (lyy_media_ads_device_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX media_ads_device_idx ON adempiere.lyy_media_ads_device USING btree (lyy_equipment_id, lyy_media_ads_id);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.meituan_store_apply (
    meituan_store_apply_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    meituan_store_id character varying(64),
    pay_status character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    meituan_store_order_id numeric(10,0),
    apply_status character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    store_name character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    lyy_district_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    district_info character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    address character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    phone_number character varying(11) NOT NULL,
    package_info jsonb,
    store_img jsonb,
    remark character varying(100),
    created timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    is_del character(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    branch_store_name character varying(20) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    business_time jsonb,
    is_await_pay character(1) DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    channel character(1) DEFAULT 0,
    bid character varying(64),
    migrate_status smallint,
    op_poi_id character varying(64),
    op_customer_id character varying(64),
    multi_point smallint,
    meituan_shop_id numeric(30,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.lyy_distributor_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.lyy_equipment_group_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.meituan_store_id is '美团店铺id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.pay_status is '支付状态：Prepare待支付，Pay已支付';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.meituan_store_order_id is '业务订单id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.apply_status is '申请状态：Applying申请中，Refuse审核不通过，Pass已通过';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.lyy_district_id is '地区id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.district_info is '地区信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.address is '商户地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.phone_number is '商户电话';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.package_info is '套餐信息数组';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.store_img is '店铺图片url数组';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.is_del is '是否删除：Y/N';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.branch_store_name is '分店名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.business_time is '营业时间：记录{开始时间:startTime,结束时间:endTime}';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.is_await_pay is '是否待支付';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.channel is '0 旧核销，1 新核销';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.bid is '美团商家Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.migrate_status is '1:已迁移美团op';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.op_poi_id is '美团op门店id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.op_customer_id is '美团op客户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.multi_point is '1:多经点位';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.meituan_store_apply.meituan_shop_id is '美团门店shop_id';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.meituan_store_apply is '美团店铺申请表';
CREATE INDEX idx_meituan_store_apply_op_poi_id ON adempiere.meituan_store_apply USING btree (op_poi_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_meituan_store_apply_district_id ON adempiere.meituan_store_apply USING btree (lyy_district_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX meituan_store_apply_pkey ON adempiere.meituan_store_apply USING btree (meituan_store_apply_id);
CREATE INDEX meituan_store_apply_group_status_idx ON adempiere.meituan_store_apply USING btree (lyy_equipment_group_id, apply_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_meituan_store_apply_lyy_distributor_id ON adempiere.meituan_store_apply USING btree (lyy_distributor_id);
```

```sql
-- 【lyy_media_ads_device】查询兑币机下发广告记录
select * from lyy_media_ads_device where lyy_media_ads_device_id in (158466,158464)

-- 【MEITUAN_STORE_APPLY】查询美团授权记录
select
* 
from 
MEITUAN_STORE_APPLY 
where lyy_distributor_id = 2149938 
and apply_status = 'Pass' and pay_status='Pay' AND is_del = 'N' limit 1
```

**库：`massage_chair`**（1 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `coins_saver_order` | 查询机器存币记录 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE coins_saver_order (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    order_id character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    equipment_value character varying(40) NOT NULL,
    equipment_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    store_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_type_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_type_value character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    coins numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    user_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    device_status smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    order_status smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    description character varying(512),
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    equity_type smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.order_id is '业务单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.equipment_value is '设备编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.equipment_id is '设备id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.store_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.equipment_type_id is '设备类型id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.equipment_type_value is '设备类型标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.coins is '存分数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.user_id is '用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.device_status is '设备状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.order_status is '订单状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.description is '描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN coins_saver_order.equity_type is '权益类型 1:积分';
COMMENT ON TABLE coins_saver_order is '存分业务订单表';
CREATE INDEX idx_coins_saver_order_uid_ev_utime ON coins_saver_order USING btree (user_id, equipment_value, device_status, updated);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX coins_saver_order_pkey ON coins_saver_order USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX saver_equipment_id_nox ON coins_saver_order USING btree (equipment_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_coins_saver_order_smctime ON coins_saver_order USING btree (store_id, merchant_id, created DESC);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX coins_saver_order_order_id_equipment_value_uindex ON coins_saver_order USING btree (order_id, equipment_value);
```

```sql
-- 【coins_saver_order】查询机器存币记录
SELECT * FROM (select
coins as "币数",
user_id as "用户id",
equipment_value as "设备编码",
created as "创建时间",
updated as "上报时间",
case when device_status =0 then '取消存币' 
when device_status =1 then '启动成功'
when device_status =2 then '同步结束状态'
when device_status =3 then '正常结束' end as "设备状态"
from coins_saver_order 
where merchant_id = 1878870
order by created desc) as t LIMIT 10000
```

### 多金宝

**库：`small_venue`**（1 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `t_merchant_card` | 多金宝查询某个会员卡号信息 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE t_merchant_card (
    id integer NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(10,0),
    code character varying(100),
    card_no character varying(50),
    create_time date,
    update_time date,
    deleted smallint,
    remark character varying(50),
    status smallint,
    password character varying(255),
    out_trade_no character varying(50),
    initial_card_no character varying(50),
    card_id character varying(50),
    store_id bigint,
    user_id bigint,
    UNIQUE (code, card_no)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.code is '商户编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.card_no is '会员卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.deleted is '是否删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.status is '卡状态：1.待制作，2.已制卡，3.已绑定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.password is '卡密码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.out_trade_no is '订单编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.initial_card_no is '初始卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.card_id is '卡id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.store_id is '开卡门店';
COMMENT ON COLUMN t_merchant_card.user_id is '用户id';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX t_merchant_card_pkey ON t_merchant_card USING btree (id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX key_code_card ON t_merchant_card USING btree (code, card_no);
CREATE INDEX idx_card_card_no ON t_merchant_card USING btree (card_no);
CREATE INDEX idx_card_merchant_code ON t_merchant_card USING btree (code);
CREATE INDEX idx_card_merchant_id ON t_merchant_card USING hash (merchant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_merchant_card_ono ON t_merchant_card USING btree (out_trade_no);
```

```sql
select * from t_merchant_card where merchant_id = 1596190 and card_no = 'LYYDJB100080';
```

### 充电桩

**库：`master`**（5 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `merchant_equipment` | 商家设备表 |
| `agent_equipment` | 代理商设备表 |
| `ad_org` | 查询商家ID |
| `ad_org_attach` | 查询是否资金池商家 |
| `equipment_charging_type` | 场地配置信息 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE adempiere.merchant_equipment (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0),
    factory_id numeric(12,0),
    equipment_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_value character varying(40) NOT NULL,
    unique_code character varying(32),
    equipment_type_id numeric(12,0),
    store_id numeric(12,0),
    equipment_name character varying(120),
    remarks character varying(256),
    register_date timestamp without time zone,
    group_number numeric(10,0),
    status character varying(12),
    cloudcode_flag boolean,
    detail_switch boolean,
    auto_refund boolean,
    show_charging boolean DEFAULT false,
    auto_restart boolean DEFAULT false,
    open_show boolean,
    group_service_cost_way character varying(20),
    unit_price numeric(10,2),
    gift_price numeric(10,2),
    display_mode smallint DEFAULT 0,
    stock integer DEFAULT 0,
    charge_before_pay_status boolean,
    charge_before_pay_min_money numeric(5,2),
    charge_before_pay_max_money numeric(5,2),
    distributorpart numeric(10,4),
    factorypart numeric(10,4),
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updatedby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    is_active boolean DEFAULT true,
    description character varying(255),
    amount numeric(6,2) DEFAULT 0,
    mer_ble_switch_value boolean,
    server_compute_prop character varying(2000),
    register_system character varying(12),
    city_id numeric,
    district_id numeric,
    address character varying(256),
    sim_longitude numeric(10,7),
    sim_latitude numeric(10,7),
    login_flag numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    business_type smallint,
    firmware_version character varying(32) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    device_type character varying(10),
    business_version_no character varying(32),
    factory_brand character varying(32),
    initial_time timestamp without time zone,
    firmware_business_no character varying(64),
    gateway smallint,
    third_factory character varying(20),
    protocol_id character varying(20),
    mac character varying(60),
    terminal_value character varying(64),
    imei_value character varying(64),
    third_platform character varying(20),
    ant_flag boolean,
    ant_mode smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.id is '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.merchant_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.factory_id is '工厂ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.unique_code is '设备唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.equipment_type_id is '设备类型ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.store_id is '店铺/场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.equipment_name is '设备名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.remarks is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.register_date is '注册时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.group_number is '机台号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.status is '状态 1.注册 2.禁用 3.解绑';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.cloudcode_flag is '是否注册到云码 0-否 1-是';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.detail_switch is '商品详情开关 0-关闭 1-开启';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.auto_refund is '是否自动退款 0=不自动退款，非0表示自动退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.show_charging is '是否显示充电倒计时页面 0 否 1 是';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.auto_restart is '是否断电自动重启 0=不自动重启，1=自动重启';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.open_show is '只针对洗衣机,是否显示开启暂停终止功能的控制开关 1是显示';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.group_service_cost_way is '计费模式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.unit_price is '启动单价';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.gift_price is '礼品单价';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.display_mode is '商品展示方式 0-按商品顺序展示 1-按货道顺序同类商品合并展示 2-按货道顺序(不合并)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.stock is '库存数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.charge_before_pay_status is '先充后付状态 0:关闭,1:打开';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.charge_before_pay_min_money is '最低余额使用限制(最小为1)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.charge_before_pay_max_money is '最高余额使用限制';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.distributorpart is '商家分成比例';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.factorypart is '广告工厂分成比例';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.createdby is '创建者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.updatedby is '更新者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.is_active is '是否可用 0:不可用；1:可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.description is '描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.amount is '启动金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.mer_ble_switch_value is '商家4G+蓝牙开关';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.server_compute_prop is '服务端计费属性';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.register_system is '注册系统标记：1.0 , 2.0 , 3.0';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.city_id is '城市ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.district_id is '地区id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.address is '地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.sim_longitude is 'sim经度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.sim_latitude is 'sim纬度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.login_flag is '登录标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.business_type is '1=福袋机，2=口红机，3=自动售货机，4=冰淇淋机，5=星座机，6=按摩垫1+N，7=功德箱 8=电子烟，9=兑币机, 11=豆浆机 ,12=咖啡机,13=格子机,14=混合货道机，15=萌盒机,16=综合售货机，17=汽车充电桩';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.firmware_version is '固件版本号, lyy_equipment的versionno';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.device_type is '设备类型 LYY/LYY2';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.business_version_no is '业务版本号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.factory_brand is '工厂品牌';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.initial_time is '初始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.firmware_business_no is '硬件业务版本/firmware_no';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.gateway is '网关';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.third_factory is '对接第三方工厂';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.protocol_id is '所属协议id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.mac is 'mac地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.terminal_value is '终端码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.imei_value is 'imei编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.third_platform is '第三方平台code，第三平台迁移过来的设备标记，东晙code: DJ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.ant_flag is '是否注册到蚂蚁链';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.merchant_equipment.ant_mode is '蚂蚁链补贴模式，0：重模式即单通，1：轻模式即双通';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.merchant_equipment is '商户设备表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX merchant_equipment_pk ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_ucode ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (unique_code, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_fversion ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (firmware_version);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_imei_value_index ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (imei_value);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX merchant_equipment_equipment_id_unqidx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (equipment_id);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_merchant_idx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING hash (merchant_id) WHERE (merchant_id IS NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_store_eqtype_idx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (store_id, equipment_type_id) WHERE (store_id IS NOT NULL);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX merchant_equipment_equipment_value_unqidx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (equipment_value);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_terminal_value_index ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (terminal_value);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_lower_terminal_value_idx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (lower((terminal_value)::text)) WHERE (lower((terminal_value)::text) IS NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_lower_imei_value_idx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (lower((imei_value)::text)) WHERE (lower((imei_value)::text) IS NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_device_type_idx ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (unique_code text_pattern_ops) WHERE ((device_type)::text = ANY ((ARRAY['EXT'::character varying, 'LYY2'::character varying])::text[]));
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_et_id ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (equipment_type_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_sid_mid ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (store_id, merchant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_ftbt ON adempiere.merchant_equipment USING btree (third_factory, business_type);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.agent_equipment (
    agent_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    ad_user_id numeric(10,0),
    agent_user_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    ad_org_id numeric(10,0),
    unique_code character varying(64),
    equipment_value character varying(32),
    equipment_type_name character varying(32),
    equipment_type_value character varying(16),
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updatedby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    bd_id numeric(10,0),
    secord_agent_user_id numeric(10,0),
    agent_equipment_status character varying(8),
    merchant_authority numeric(2,0) DEFAULT 1,
    remark character varying(200),
    current_agent_user_id numeric(10,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.agent_equipment.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.agent_equipment.current_agent_user_id is '设备当前所属代理商id';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX agent_equipment_pkey ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING btree (agent_equipment_id);
CREATE INDEX agent_equipment_current_agent_user_id_idx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING btree (current_agent_user_id);
CREATE INDEX agent_equipment_code_idx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING hash (unique_code);
CREATE INDEX agent_equipment_eqid_idx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING btree (lyy_equipment_id);
CREATE INDEX agent_equipment_org_idx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING btree (ad_org_id);
CREATE INDEX agent_equipment_secord_agent_user_idx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING hash (secord_agent_user_id) WHERE (secord_agent_user_id IS NOT NULL);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX agent_equipment_value_unqidx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING btree (equipment_value);
CREATE INDEX agent_equipment_user_equipment_idx ON adempiere.agent_equipment USING btree (agent_user_id, agent_equipment_id);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.ad_org (
    ad_org_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    ad_client_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    isactive character(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Y'::bpchar,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    createdby numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updatedby numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    value character varying(40) NOT NULL,
    name character varying(60) NOT NULL,
    description character varying(255),
    issummary character(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    ad_replicationstrategy_id numeric(10,0),
    is_internal_orders_enabled character(1) DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    c_bpartner_id numeric(10,0),
    balance numeric(10,2),
    total numeric(12,2),
    address character varying(256),
    alipay_account character varying(40),
    card_holder character varying(30),
    bank_account character varying(60),
    bank_name character varying(30),
    sub_bank character varying(250),
    type character varying(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::bpchar,
    default_price numeric,
    alipay_name character varying(20) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    cooperation_mode character varying(6) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    contact character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    telephone character varying(21) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    lyy_staff_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_district_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    has_income character(1) DEFAULT NULL::bpchar,
    sub_total numeric DEFAULT '0'::numeric,
    sub_merchant character(1) DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    merchant_number character varying(16) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    sub_created timestamp without time zone,
    api_key character varying(60) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    secret_key character varying(60) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    cib_total numeric DEFAULT '0'::numeric,
    cib_merchant character(1) DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    cib_number character varying(16) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    cib_key character varying(32) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    cib_created timestamp without time zone,
    isadvertise character(1) DEFAULT 'Y'::bpchar,
    min_ad_price numeric DEFAULT 0.6,
    min_equipment_count numeric(10,0) DEFAULT '300'::numeric,
    ad_percent numeric DEFAULT 0.1,
    customer_service_number character varying(20),
    business_name character varying(50),
    default_discount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 10,
    agent_number character varying(20),
    can_owe character(1),
    can_owe_fee numeric(10,2),
    agent_id numeric(10,0),
    advert_price numeric(10,2),
    distributorpart numeric(10,2),
    is_member_card_enable character(1) DEFAULT 'Y'::bpchar,
    avatar character varying(255),
    third_pay_service_code character varying(255),
    wechat_pay_flag character(1) DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    ispopadwindow character(1) DEFAULT 'N'::bpchar,
    isopenintegral character(1),
    balance_alias character(3),
    cib_balance numeric DEFAULT '0'::numeric,
    country_code character varying(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CN'::character varying,
    wechat_facepay_sign_flag character(1),
    allow_pay numeric(1,0) DEFAULT 0,
    platform_user_id numeric(10,0),
    app_id character varying(64),
    merchant_logo character varying(255),
    FOREIGN KEY (ad_replicationstrategy_id) REFERENCES ad_replicationstrategy(ad_replicationstrategy_id) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED,
    CHECK ((has_income = ANY (ARRAY['Y'::bpchar, 'N'::bpchar]))),
    CHECK ((isactive = ANY (ARRAY['Y'::bpchar, 'N'::bpchar]))),
    CHECK ((isadvertise = ANY (ARRAY['Y'::bpchar, 'N'::bpchar]))),
    CHECK ((sub_merchant = ANY (ARRAY['Y'::bpchar, 'N'::bpchar]))),
    FOREIGN KEY (ad_client_id) REFERENCES ad_client(ad_client_id) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED,
    UNIQUE (value)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.ad_org_id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.value is '编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.name is '名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.description is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.issummary is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.ad_replicationstrategy_id is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.is_internal_orders_enabled is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.c_bpartner_id is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.balance is '收益余额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.total is '收益总额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.address is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.alipay_account is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.card_holder is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.bank_account is '没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org.bank_name is '没用';

    replenish_phone character varying(255),
    customer_topic_version jsonb,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    draw_fee_limit smallint,
    third_balance_open jsonb,
    alias_name character varying(60),
    link_name character varying(30),
    primary_category character varying(128),
    is_wallet_settlement boolean DEFAULT false,
    business_scene_merchant_id numeric(11,0),
    business_scene_agent_user_id numeric(11,0),
    belong_agent_user_id numeric(11,0),
    belong_agent_merchant_id numeric(11,0),
    business_scene_type character varying(32)
);

homePageNotify：导入余币余额首页强提醒开关设置：Y-开启，N-关闭
customerServiceAccess：导入余币余额客服入口开关设置：Y-开启，N-关闭';

COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org_attach.belong_agent_user_id is '归属代理商ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org_attach.belong_agent_merchant_id is '归属商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.ad_org_attach.business_scene_type is 'dealer：经销商，brand：品牌商';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ad_org_attach_pkey ON adempiere.ad_org_attach USING btree (ad_org_attach_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ad_org_attach_org_id_index ON adempiere.ad_org_attach USING btree (ad_org_id);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.equipment_charging_type (
```

```sql
-- 【merchant_equipment】商家设备表
select * from merchant_equipment where equipment_value ='87150504'

-- 【agent_equipment】代理商设备表
select * from agent_equipment where equipment_value ='87150504'

-- 【ad_org】查询商家ID
select * from ad_org  where value like '%17665126143%'

-- 【ad_org_attach】查询是否资金池商家
select * from ad_org_attach  where ad_org_id=1093804

-- 【equipment_charging_type】场地配置信息
select * from equipment_charging_type where lyy_equipment_group_id =1120128
```

**库：`massage_chair`**（5 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `lyy_charging_order` | 充电业务订单 |
| `lyy_charging_order_power` | 充电订单对应功率过程 |
| `lyy_charging_equipment_port` | 设备端口 |
| `lyy_ic_card` | IC卡表 |
| `lyy_ic_card_flow` | IC卡流水记录 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE lyy_charging_order (
    id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(12,0),
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(12,0),
    group_service_id numeric(12,0),
    passage_way numeric(4,0),
    coins numeric(10,0),
    money numeric(12,2),
    openid character varying(128),
    lyy_user_id numeric(12,0),
    durings numeric(10,0),
    start_time timestamp without time zone,
    pause_start_time timestamp without time zone,
    pause_during_time numeric(10,0),
    device_status numeric,
    is_send_user numeric(4,0),
    order_status numeric,
    create_time timestamp without time zone,
    update_time timestamp without time zone,
    deleted numeric(4,0),
    value character varying(16),
    username character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    description character varying(255),
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0),
    refund_money numeric(10,2),
    lyy_online_coins_history_id numeric(12,0),
    main_order_id numeric(10,0),
    parent_id numeric(10,0),
    service_durings numeric(10,0),
    group_number numeric(10,0),
    actual_time numeric(10,0) DEFAULT 0,
    close_action smallint DEFAULT 0,
    out_trade_no character varying(32),
    hardware_minutes numeric(10,0),
    service_money numeric(10,2),
    login_flag numeric(10,0),
    agent_user_id numeric(11,0),
    group_service_cost_way character varying(20),
    device_status_name character varying(30),
    refund_type smallint,
    limit_time smallint,
    refund_way numeric(1,0),
    time_or_electric numeric(10,0),
    ic_card_no character varying(32),
    attach_info character varying(3000),
    end_power integer,
    pay_score_type smallint,
    start_type smallint,
    extend_param character varying(500),
    app_id character varying(128),
    pay_order_no character varying(64),
    order_no character varying(32),
    pay_way smallint,
    delay_confirm_time timestamp without time zone,
    delay_flag smallint,
    delay_status smallint,
    extra_param text
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (create_time);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.id is '订单ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.lyy_equipment_group_id is '场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.group_service_id is '套餐ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.passage_way is '通道';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.coins is '模拟投币数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.money is '价格';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.openid is '用户openid';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.lyy_user_id is '用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.durings is '时长';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.start_time is '订单开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.pause_start_time is '暂时开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.pause_during_time is '暂停多少时长';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.device_status is '设备状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.is_send_user is '是否发送用户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.order_status is '订单状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.update_time is '更新时间/结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.deleted is '是否删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.username is '用户昵称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.description is '描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.refund_money is '退款金额 ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.lyy_online_coins_history_id is '消费记录总表id-废弃';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.main_order_id is '主订单id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.parent_id is '上一次订单id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.service_durings is '服务套餐时长,单位:分钟';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.group_number is '设备机台号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.actual_time is '实际充电时长';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.close_action is '订单关闭来源, 0正常关闭，1商家远程停止, 2用户手动结束, 3设备启动失败, 4设备故障, 5订单未激活, 6订单服务时长为空, 7同一通道号重复下单关闭 8设备或端口异常，被迫停止 9瞬时功率过大，停止充电 10未接充电器 11机箱内出现烟雾，电源关闭 12充电过程中出现短路故障 13充电过程中出现过载故障 14设备意外断电或者意外复位导致停止充电';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.out_trade_no is '支付订单编号   0：余额启动，1：月卡启动,其他值 有支付订单 ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.hardware_minutes is '主板剩余时长(分钟)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.service_money is '服务套餐金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.login_flag is '登录标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.agent_user_id is '代理商ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.group_service_cost_way is '计费模式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.device_status_name is '上报状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.refund_type is '退款规则：0:不退款，1:实时退款，2限时退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.limit_time is '限时退款 限制时间（分钟）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.refund_way is '退款方式：（1退余额，2原路退款）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.time_or_electric is '电量或者时长';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.ic_card_no is 'ic卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.attach_info is '附加信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.end_power is '充电功率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.pay_score_type is '支付分状态:-1不存在,0未支付,1待支付,2已支付,3已取消,4已失效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.start_type is '启动方式: 0余额启动,1:月卡启动, 2:投币, 3:刷卡,4:支付启动 ,5:支付分启动, 6:先充后付,7:服务端时长套餐，8:服务端金额套餐';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.extend_param is '订单扩展信息，用于显示';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.app_id is '微信appid(支付分需要回传)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.pay_order_no is '支付订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.order_no is '新支付订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.pay_way is '支付方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.delay_confirm_time is '延迟结算订单确认时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.delay_flag is '是否走延迟结算标识，0:否，1:是';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.delay_status is '延迟结算状态，0：待确认，1：已确认，2：确认失败，3：无结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order.extra_param is '额外一些参数';

CREATE TABLE lyy_charging_order_power (
    id numeric(12,0),
    charging_order_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    charging_time character varying(8) NOT NULL,
    charging_power numeric(8,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    charging_electric numeric(9,5),
    charging_temperature numeric(10,2),
    charging_voltage numeric(10,2),
    charging_current numeric(10,2),
    created timestamp(6) without time zone
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (create_time);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.id is '充电功率记录ID（主键）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_order_id is '充电订单ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_time is '充电时间点（HH：SS 格式）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_power is '充电功率(网络原因不能够上传默认为0)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_electric is '充电电量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_temperature is '温度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_voltage is '电压';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_order_power.charging_current is '电流';

CREATE TABLE lyy_charging_equipment_port (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id bigint NOT NULL,
    station_id bigint NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id bigint NOT NULL,
    unique_code character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    mode_type smallint DEFAULT 0,
    login_flag integer,
    port smallint NOT NULL,
    port_status smallint,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    created_by character varying(32),
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated_by character varying(32),
    port_operator_status smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.id is '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.merchant_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.station_id is '站点ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.unique_code is '设备唯一编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.mode_type is '0、普通设备，统计端口，1：脉冲设备，不统计端口';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.login_flag is '登陆标识、冗余字段';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.port is '端口号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.port_status is '端口状态
IDLE(1, "空闲"),
CHARGING(2, "充电中"),
FAULT(3, "故障"),
DISABLE(4, "禁用"),
OFFLINE(5, "离线"),
UN_KNOW(255, "未知"),
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_charging_equipment_port.created_by is '创建人';

CREATE TABLE lyy_ic_card (
    lyy_ic_card_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    merchant_id numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    factory_id numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    card_no character varying(32) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    amount numeric(8,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    modify_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    memo character varying(256) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    user_name character varying(64),
    phone numeric(11,0),
    status smallint DEFAULT 1,
    type smallint DEFAULT 0,
    user_id numeric(12,0),
    lyy_group_id numeric(11,0),
    school_id numeric(10,0),
    class_id numeric(10,0),
    sub_type smallint,
    contact_name character varying(32),
    charge_mode character varying(64),
    output_water_type character varying(64),
    single_water_amount numeric(6,0),
    water_work_mode smallint,
    bind_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    expiration_date date,
    present numeric(8,2),
    remark character varying(32),
    third_user_id character varying(32),
    third_platform character varying(16) DEFAULT '0'::character varying,
    grade_id numeric(10,0),
    limit_status smallint,
    limit_type smallint,
    prestore_status numeric(4,0),
    support_member_card smallint,
    charging_count_limit smallint,
    day_limit_amount numeric(11,2),
    month_limit_amount numeric(11,2)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.lyy_ic_card_id is '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.merchant_id is 'ad_org表的用户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.factory_id is '工厂ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.card_no is '卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.amount is '卡余额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.modify_time is '修改时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.memo is '说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.user_name is '用户姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.phone is '手机号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.status is '卡状态：0-禁用，1-正常，2-挂失，3-未绑定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.type is ' 卡类型，默认为0 0-离线卡，1-在线卡';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.lyy_group_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.school_id is '学校ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.class_id is '班级ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.sub_type is '1为水杯，2为ic卡';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.charge_mode is '计费方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.output_water_type is '出水方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.single_water_amount is '单次取水量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.water_work_mode is '售水机工作方式(1 在线 2离线)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.bind_time is '绑定时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.expiration_date is '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.present is '赠送金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.third_user_id is '第三方平台ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.third_platform is '第三方平台枚举 0:乐摇摇 DJ:东晙';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.grade_id is '年级ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.limit_status is '限制状态 0=未限制 1=已限额 2=手动解除限额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.limit_type is '限制类型 0= 日限额 1= 月限额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.prestore_status is '预存状态:0待领取,1已领取';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.support_member_card is '支持会员卡类型：0或null=不支持，1=次卡';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.month_limit_amount is '月使用限制金额';

CREATE TABLE lyy_ic_card_flow (
    lyy_ic_card_flow_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_no character(16),
    lyy_user_id numeric(11,0),
    merchant_id numeric(11,0),
    card_no character varying(32),
    flow_amount numeric(8,2),
    flow_type numeric(2,0) NOT NULL,
    ext character varying(64),
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_group character varying(50),
    memo character varying(64),
    status numeric(1,0),
    amount numeric(8,2),
    receive_status numeric(1,0),
    refund_amount numeric(8,2),
    refund_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    expiration_date date,
    time_limit bigint,
    usage_amount numeric(8,0),
    charge_mode character varying(64),
    order_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_ic_card_id numeric(11,0),
    present numeric(8,2),
    flow_present numeric(8,2),
    passway numeric(4,0),
    start_param character varying(1024),
    deduction_remarks character varying(64),
    actual_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    order_no character varying(64),
    update_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    refund_present numeric(8,2)
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (create_time);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_ic_card_flow_id is '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.equipment_no is '设备号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.merchant_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.card_no is '卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.flow_amount is '流水记录金额，单位：元';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.flow_type is ' 1扫码充卡 2刷卡充电 3使用账户余额充卡 4回刷停止充电 5充电自动停止 6-扫码充卡结果 7-打卡洗衣 8-刷卡洗衣启动失败 9-剩余费用退款,10 实际消费金额 ,11 刷卡取水,12手工退款 13销卡,14余额转入,15商家赠送,16销卡退款,17商家修改增加余额,18商家修改减少余额, 19消费扣款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.ext is '扩展字段和流水类型匹配，对应充值订单号、补卡的卡号
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_equipment_group is '设备归属的场地名称可改变';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.memo is '说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.status is '0初始状态 
1操作成功  
2失败 
3超时 
4无效 -1已退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.amount is 'ic卡当前的余额，计算后的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.receive_status is '圈存版充值流水领取状态：0-待领取；1-领取中；2-已领取';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.refund_amount is '充值余额-退款金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.refund_time is '退款时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.expiration_date is '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.time_limit is '有效期（天）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.usage_amount is '使用量(售水机为出水量 ml)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.charge_mode is '计费方式（TIME_INTERVAL-按时段计费，TIMES-按次数计费）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.order_id is '业务订单Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_ic_card_id is 'ic卡ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.present is 'ic卡当前的赠送余额，计算后的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.flow_present is '赠送金额流水';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.passway is '端口';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.start_param is '启动参数(参考:LifeOrderStartParamDTO)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.deduction_remarks is '扣款备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.actual_time is '消费时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.order_no is '中台支付单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.refund_present is '赠送余额-退款金额';
COMMENT ON TABLE lyy_ic_card_flow is '卡流水金额';
```

```sql
-- 【lyy_charging_order】充电业务订单
select * from lyy_charging_order where out_trade_no ='PA303026101011333322000143141005'

-- 【lyy_charging_order_power】充电订单对应功率过程
select * from lyy_charging_order_power where charging_order_id =1285629

-- 【lyy_charging_equipment_port】设备端口
select * from lyy_charging_equipment_port where unique_code='0000000087150504'

-- 【lyy_ic_card】IC卡表
select * from lyy_ic_card where card_no ='2788463341' and merchant_id =1036030

-- 【lyy_ic_card_flow】IC卡流水记录
select * from lyy_ic_card_flow where card_no ='2788463341' order by create_time  desc
```

**库：`advert`**（8 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card` | 保险周卡、保险月卡、V会员保险月卡、星会员保险月卡 |
| `lyy_cdz_insurance_order` | 投保订单记录 |
| `lyy_cdz_activity_black_list` | 用户、场地、商家黑名单 |
| `cdz_debit_order` | 星会员签约表 |
| `automatic_debit_order` | V会员签约表 |
| `lyy_cdz_member_card` | V会员、星会员卡表 |
| `lyy_cdz_insurance_equipment_order_detail` | 设备整桩投保记录 |
| `lyy_distributor_advertise_coupon` | 用户广告红包权益 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card (
    lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_user_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_type_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    out_trade_no character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    third_trxid character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    status numeric(2,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    pay_amount numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    purchase_time timestamp without time zone,
    ad_org_id numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    ad_split_type numeric(2,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    distributor_amount numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_amount numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    unionid character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    name character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    invalid_flag numeric(2,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    invalid_date timestamp without time zone,
    trade_type character varying(12) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    tip_flag character varying(1) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    order_type character varying(2) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    updatedby numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    card_type integer DEFAULT 1,
    effect_date timestamp without time zone,
    complaint_flag character varying(2),
    complaint_remark character varying(200),
    refund_type smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card_id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.lyy_user_id is '用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.lyy_distributor_id is '商家id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.lyy_equipment_id is '设备id';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.out_trade_no is '乐摇摇订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.third_trxid is '第三方订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.status is '状态：1-待支付，2-已支付，3-自动退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.pay_amount is '实际支付金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.purchase_time is '支付成功时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.ad_org_id is '支付虚拟商户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.ad_split_type is '类型：5-充电桩保险月卡';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.distributor_amount is '商户分得总额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.lyy_amount is '乐摇摇分得总额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.unionid is '玩家unionid';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.name is '玩家昵称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.invalid_flag is '是否失效：0-否；1-是';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.invalid_date is '失效时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.trade_type is '支付渠道：aliPay-支付宝；JSAPI-微信';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.tip_flag is '是否已弹窗提示:0-否;1-是';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.order_type is '订单来源:1-banner弹窗;2扫码弹窗;3-购物车弹窗';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.createdby is '创建人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.updatedby is '更新人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.card_type is '卡类别：1月卡；2-周卡';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.effect_date is '生效时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.complaint_flag is '是否投诉：0-否；1-是';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card.refund_type is '退款来源：1-平台（默认）；2-用户';
COMMENT ON TABLE lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card is '充电桩保险购卡表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_lyy_lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card_new ON lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card USING btree (lyy_cdz_insurance_buy_card_id);

CREATE TABLE lyy_cdz_insurance_order (
    lyy_cdz_insurance_order_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_user_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_type_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    out_trade_no character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    third_trxid character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    status numeric(2,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    pay_amount numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    purchase_time timestamp without time zone,
    ad_org_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    insurance_amount numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    policy_no character varying(128) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    total_actual_premium numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    total_insured_amount numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    insurance_begin_date timestamp without time zone,
    insurance_end_date timestamp without time zone,
    insurance_status character varying(2) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    insurance_describe character varying(128) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    ad_split_type numeric(2,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    order_type character varying(2) DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    tip_flag character varying(2) DEFAULT '0'::character varying,
    trade_type character varying(12) DEFAULT 'JSAPI'::character varying,
    distributor_amount numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_amount numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    unionid character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    name character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    updatedby numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    insurance_type character varying(2) DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    openid character varying(64),
    equipment_value character varying(64),
    order_source character varying(2) DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    settlement_flag character varying(2),
    insure_platform smallint
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (created);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.lyy_cdz_insurance_order_id is '主键id';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.lyy_equipment_group_id is '设备场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.out_trade_no is '乐摇摇订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.third_trxid is '第三方订单号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.purchase_time is '支付成功时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.ad_org_id is '支付虚拟商户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.insurance_amount is '保单金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.policy_no is '保单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.total_actual_premium is '保费';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_cdz_insurance_order.insurance_describe is '描述';

CREATE TABLE cdz_debit_order (
    id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    order_no character varying(64),
    user_id numeric(11,0),
    distributor_id numeric(11,0),
    group_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_value character varying(32),
    equipment_type numeric(11,0),
    client character varying(16),
    app_id character varying(64),
    debit_type smallint,
    phone_number character varying(32),
    first_pay_amount numeric(10,2),
    debit_amount numeric(10,2),
    last_debit_amount numeric(10,2),
    execute_time character varying(32),
    period character varying(32),
    sign_status smallint,
    deduction_status smallint,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    createdby bigint,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    updatedby bigint,
    channel_order_no character varying(64),
    extra_info jsonb,
    business_order_no character varying(64),
    last_debit_date character varying(32),
    fail_reason character varying(200),
    platform_user_id character varying(64),
    debit_config_id numeric(11,0),
    channel_code character varying(32),
    next_deduct_time character varying(32),
    unsign_time character varying(32),
    contract_id character varying(64),
    fail_count smallint,
    deleted boolean DEFAULT false
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.user_id is '用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.distributor_id is '商家id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.group_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.equipment_id is '设备id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.equipment_type is '设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.client is '客户端';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.debit_type is '扣费方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.phone_number is '手机号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.first_pay_amount is '首笔金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.debit_amount is '扣费金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.execute_time is '执行时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.period is '执行间隔时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.sign_status is ' 签约状态 0-未处理,1-签约成功,2-签约取消,3-签约失败';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.deduction_status is ' 扣款状态 0-未处理,1-扣款成功,2-扣款处理中,3-扣款失败';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.channel_order_no is '渠道订单号（签约）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.extra_info is '透传信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.business_order_no is '业务订单号（扣款）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.last_debit_date is '最近扣款时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.fail_reason is '失败原因';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.platform_user_id is '第三方平台用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.channel_code is '渠道编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.next_deduct_time is '下次扣费时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.unsign_time is '解约时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.contract_id is '委托代扣协议id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN cdz_debit_order.fail_count is '扣费失败重试次数';

CREATE TABLE lyy_cdz_member_card (

    order_position smallint,
    agent_user_id bigint,
    agent_user_name character varying(64),
    user_phone character varying(32),
    start_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    end_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    discount_rate integer,
    single_limit_amount numeric(10,2),
    month_limit_amount numeric(10,2),
    current_limit_amount numeric(10,2),
    callback_json character varying(500),

    createdby character varying(64),
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
```

**库：`commercial_charges`**（4 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `system_charge_equipment_bill` | 收费规则 |
| `system_charge_equipment_bill_flow` | 收费流水 |
| `system_charge_equipment` | 收费设备 |
| `system_charge_config` | 收费配置 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE system_charge_equipment_bill (
    system_charge_equipment_bill_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    cost numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    increase_price numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    charge_days integer NOT NULL,
    total_fee numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    balance_fee numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    end_date date NOT NULL,
    next_end_date date NOT NULL,
    status integer NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    charge_type integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    charge_value integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 6000,
    factory_increase_price_rate numeric(10,2),
    system_charge_rule_id numeric(10,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.cost is '成本价（分）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.increase_price is '提价（分）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.charge_days is '续费天数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.total_fee is '缴费总额（分）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.balance_fee is '剩余缴费金额（分）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.end_date is '当前到期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.next_end_date is '续费后到期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.status is '状态：0未缴完；1已缴完';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.charge_type is '收费类型: 0:按台收费,1:按GMV抽成收费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.charge_value is '收费值-如果是比例，则是万分比，如果是固定金额则单位是分';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.factory_increase_price_rate is '工厂分成比例';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill.system_charge_rule_id is '规则ID';
COMMENT ON TABLE system_charge_equipment_bill is '设备缴费单表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_pkey ON system_charge_equipment_bill USING btree (system_charge_equipment_bill_id);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_equipment_idx ON system_charge_equipment_bill USING btree (lyy_equipment_id, end_date, status);

CREATE TABLE system_charge_equipment_bill_flow (
    system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    system_charge_equipment_bill_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    out_trade_no character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    channel_code smallint NOT NULL,
    merchant_code character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    time_end timestamp(6) without time zone,
    payment_amount numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    amount numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    ratio numeric(4,2) NOT NULL,
    status integer NOT NULL,
    agent_user_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    from_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    from_equipment_group_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    from_distributor_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    from_agent_user_id numeric(10,0),
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.system_charge_equipment_bill_id is '设备缴费单表ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.out_trade_no is '支付订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.channel_code is '支付渠道号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.merchant_code is '支付商户号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.time_end is '支付完成时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.payment_amount is '支付金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.amount is '流水金额（分）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.ratio is '扣减比例';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.status is '状态：0未支付；1待支付；2已支付；3退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.agent_user_id is '代理商ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.lyy_distributor_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.from_equipment_id is '来源设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.from_equipment_group_id is '来源场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.from_distributor_id is '来源商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment_bill_flow.from_agent_user_id is '来源代理商ID';
COMMENT ON TABLE system_charge_equipment_bill_flow is '设备缴费流水表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_pkey ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING btree (system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_id);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_distributor_time_end_idx ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING btree (lyy_distributor_id, time_end);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_distributor_created_idx ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING btree (created, lyy_distributor_id);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_out_trade_no_idx ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING hash (out_trade_no);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_distributor_idx ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING btree (from_distributor_id, time_end);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_bill_equipment_bill_idx ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING hash (system_charge_equipment_bill_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_system_charge_equipment_bill_flow_stauts ON system_charge_equipment_bill_flow USING btree (status);

CREATE TABLE system_charge_equipment (
    system_charge_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    system_charge_rule_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    agent_user_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_equipment_type_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_value character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    first_registration_date timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    end_date date NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    flow_card_end_date timestamp(6) without time zone,
    is_confirm smallint DEFAULT 0
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.system_charge_rule_id is '对应系统收费规则表ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.agent_user_id is '代理商ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.lyy_distributor_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.lyy_equipment_group_id is '场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.lyy_equipment_type_id is '设备类型ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.first_registration_date is '设备首次注册时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.end_date is '设备过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.flow_card_end_date is '流量卡到期时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_equipment.is_confirm is '是否确认(0:未确认,1:已确认)';
COMMENT ON TABLE system_charge_equipment is '系统收费设备表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX charge_equipment_combination_pkey ON system_charge_equipment USING btree (system_charge_equipment_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX system_charge_equipment_unqidx ON system_charge_equipment USING btree (lyy_equipment_id);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_agent_idx ON system_charge_equipment USING btree (agent_user_id, end_date);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_distributor_idx ON system_charge_equipment USING btree (lyy_distributor_id, end_date);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_equipment_idx ON system_charge_equipment USING btree (lyy_equipment_id, end_date);
CREATE INDEX system_charge_equipment_value_idx ON system_charge_equipment USING hash (equipment_value);

CREATE TABLE system_charge_config (
    system_charge_config_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_equipment_type_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    agent_user_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    cost numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    status numeric(2,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    customer_type integer NOT NULL,
    charge_days integer NOT NULL,
    charge_type numeric(2,0) NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updatedby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.system_charge_config_id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.lyy_equipment_type_id is '设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.agent_user_id is '代理商ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.lyy_distributor_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.cost is '成本价(分)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.status is '状态   0：生效  1：无效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.customer_type is '客户类型,总控(1),白名单(2)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.charge_days is '续费天数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.charge_type is '收费类型: 0:按台收费,1:按GMV抽成收费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.createdby is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.updatedby is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN system_charge_config.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON TABLE system_charge_config is '系统收费配置表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX system_charge_customer_pkey ON system_charge_config USING btree (system_charge_config_id);
```
