### 汽充桩

**库：`carcharging`**（4 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `infra_station` | 站点 |
| `order_charge` | 订单 |
| `family_equipment_vip` | 家充设备活动表 |
| `member_user_info` | 用户表 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE infra_station (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    operator_code character(9),
    platform_origin_id integer DEFAULT 1,
    merchant_id bigint NOT NULL,
    station_id bigint NOT NULL,
    business_hours_type integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    business_hours_desc character varying(255),
    station_tel character varying(50),
    service_tel character varying(50),
    park_num integer,
    park_fee_desc character varying(255),
    guide character varying(255),
    invoice_type integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    images character varying,
    station_name character varying(50) NOT NULL,
    province_id numeric NOT NULL,
    city_id numeric(10,2) NOT NULL,
    district_id numeric NOT NULL,
    district_code character(12) NOT NULL,
    address character varying(255) NOT NULL,
    station_operate_status smallint NOT NULL,
    lng numeric(12,8),
    lat numeric(12,8),
    remark character varying(255),
    station_visible smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    price_model_id bigint,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by bigint NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    updated_by bigint,
    organization_id bigint,
    deleted boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    ext_resource text,
    platform_origin_merchant_id character varying(64),
    station_type integer DEFAULT 0,
    hlht_platform_operator_id character varying(32),
    hlht_merchant_operator_id character varying(32),
    hlht_station_id character varying(32),
    hlht_extend_info text
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.id is '主键Id，雪花算法生成';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.operator_code is '组织机构代码，去掉前八位和最后一位';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.platform_origin_id is '平台来源Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.merchant_id is '商户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.station_id is '关联中台站场Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.business_hours_type is '运营时间类型
0：7*24小时
1：非7*24小时';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.business_hours_desc is '运营时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.station_tel is '站点电话';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.service_tel is '服务电话；平台服务电话';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.park_num is '车位数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.park_fee_desc is '停车费用描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.guide is '站点引导，描述性文字';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.invoice_type is '开票方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.images is '站场图片';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.station_name is '站场名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.province_id is '省Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.city_id is '城市Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.district_id is '区域Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.district_code is '区域行政编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.address is '地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.station_operate_status is '站场运营状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.lng is '经度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.lat is '纬度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.station_visible is 'App可见不可见
INVISIBLE(0, "不可见")
VISIBLE(1, "可见")';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.price_model_id is '最新计费模型Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.updated is '修改时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.organization_id is '组织Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.deleted is '软删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.ext_resource is '特殊资源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.platform_origin_merchant_id is '平台来源商户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.station_type is '场地类型，0：默认，1：支持校园一卡通，2：支持并充';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.hlht_platform_operator_id is '互联互通平台组织机构代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.hlht_merchant_operator_id is '互联互通商户组织机构代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.hlht_station_id is '互联互通第三方场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN infra_station.hlht_extend_info is '互联互通推送站点额外信息json格式';
COMMENT ON TABLE infra_station is '充电站基础信息';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX station_pk ON infra_station USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX infra_station_station_id_deleted_index ON infra_station USING btree (station_id DESC, deleted);

CREATE TABLE order_charge (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    member_id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_member_id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id bigint NOT NULL,
    out_trade_id character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    pay_id character varying(32),
    out_side_order_no character varying(64),
    station_id bigint NOT NULL,
    equipment_code character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    socket_code character varying(22) NOT NULL,
    socket_num integer NOT NULL,
    unique_code character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    pay_mode smallint,
    total_amount numeric(15,2),
    actual_amount numeric(15,2),
    reduce_amount numeric(15,2),
    total_charge_fee numeric(15,2),
    total_service_fee numeric(15,2),
    total_power numeric(15,4),
    total_time integer,
    end_reason character varying(255),
    pay_status smallint,
    order_status smallint NOT NULL,
    order_origin smallint NOT NULL,
    order_mode smallint NOT NULL,
    station_name character varying(64),
    pay_time timestamp without time zone,
    price_model_id bigint NOT NULL,
    equipment_statute_type bigint NOT NULL,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by bigint NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    updated_by bigint,
    organization_id bigint,
    deleted boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    nickname character varying(50),
    price_data text,
    open_id character varying(64),
    app_id character varying(64),
    start_amount numeric(15,2),
    start_mode smallint,
    finish_time timestamp without time zone,
    license_plate character varying(20),
    platform_id character varying(15) DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    operator_id character varying(15),
    equipment_id bigint,
    equipment_type integer,
    card_no character varying(30),
    order_mode_param numeric(15,2),
    es_flag boolean DEFAULT false,
    pay_version smallint DEFAULT 1,
    pay_need boolean DEFAULT false,
    delay_status integer,
    end_reason_code character varying(20),
    normal_flag integer,
    hlht_socket_code character varying(40),
    car_team_id bigint,
    car_team_name character varying(30),
    vin_code character varying(32),
    order_type integer,
    hlht_platform_operator_id character varying(20),
    hlht_merchant_operator_id character varying(20),
    delay_flag integer
) WITH (fillfactor=100);
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.id is '主键Id，雪花算法生成';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.member_id is '会员Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.merchant_member_id is '商户会员Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.merchant_id is '商户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.out_trade_id is '交易单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.pay_id is '支付单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.out_side_order_no is '第三方交易订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.station_id is '站id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.equipment_code is '充电桩编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.socket_code is '充电枪编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.socket_num is '枪序号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.unique_code is '设备唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.pay_mode is '
    WECHAT("wechat", 1, "Wechat"),
    WX_MINI("wxMini", 2, "Wechat"),
    ALI_PAY("aliPay", 3, "Alipay"),
    ALI_MINI("aliMini", 4, "Alipay"),
    BALANCED("Balanced", 90, "Balanced"),
    UNKNOWN("Unknown", 99, "Unknown"),
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.total_amount is '总金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.actual_amount is '实付金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.reduce_amount is '优惠金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.total_charge_fee is '总电费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.total_service_fee is '总服务费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.total_power is '总充电量，单位度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.total_time is '总充电时长，单位分钟';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.end_reason is '结束原因';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.pay_status is 'UN_PAY(1, 待支付),
PAYING(2, 支付中),
PAY_SUCCESS(3, 支付成功),
PAY_FAIL(4, 支付失败),
PAY_CANCEL(5, "支付取消"),;
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.order_status is 'RUNNING(1,"启动中"),
RUN_FAIL(2,"启动失败"),
CHARGING(3,"（启动成功）充电中"),
PEND(4,"(停止失败)结算中"),
FINISH(5,"充电完成"),
;';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.order_origin is 'customer(1, "c端"),
card(2, "刷卡"),
vin(3, "vin码"),';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.order_mode is 'AUTO(1,"自动充满"),
money(2,"按金额"),
ele(3,"按电量"),
time(4,"按时间"),';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.station_name is '场地名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.pay_time is '支付时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.price_model_id is '当时使用的计费模型id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.equipment_statute_type is '充电规约类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.created_by is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.updated is '结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.organization_id is '组织Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.deleted is '软删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.nickname is '用户昵称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.price_data is '价格数据（冗余计费模型）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.open_id is '微信openid';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.app_id is '微信的appid';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.start_amount is '启动金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.start_mode is '启动方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.finish_time is '完结时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.license_plate is '车牌号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.platform_id is '平台ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.operator_id is '商户组织机构代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.equipment_id is '中台的设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.equipment_type is '设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.card_no is '刷卡启动的卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.order_mode_param is '充电方式参数
自动充满为空
按时长充电：是时长
按电量充电：是电量
按金额充电：是金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.es_flag is 'es标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.pay_version is '支付版本，1-旧支付，2-新支付';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.pay_need is '是否需要拉起支付标识，true是，false否';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.delay_status is '延迟结算状态 0-待确认 1-成功 2-失败 3-无结果 999-确认异常';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.end_reason_code is '结束原因编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.normal_flag is '是否正常单表示，0：正常单，1：异常单';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.hlht_socket_code is '互联互通枪编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.car_team_id is '车队ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.car_team_name is '车队名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.vin_code is 'vin码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.order_type is '2：放电订单，为空是充电订单';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.hlht_platform_operator_id is '外部设备平台id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.hlht_merchant_operator_id is '外部设备商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN order_charge.delay_flag is '延迟结算标识';
COMMENT ON TABLE order_charge is '充电订单表';
CREATE INDEX idx_order_charge_eftime ON order_charge USING btree (equipment_code, finish_time DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_order_charge_member_id ON order_charge USING btree (member_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_lyy_order_charge ON order_charge USING btree (id);

CREATE INDEX order_charge_tmpmerchant_order_status_index ON order_charge USING btree (merchant_id, order_status);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX order_charge_tmp_out_trade_id_uindex ON order_charge USING btree (out_trade_id);
CREATE INDEX order_charge_tmp_pay_id_index ON order_charge USING btree (pay_id);
CREATE INDEX order_charge_tmp_socket_code_created_index ON order_charge USING btree (socket_code, created);

CREATE TABLE family_equipment_vip (

);
```

### 中台

**库：`commercial_charges`**（8 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `merchant_product` | saas、ai经营分析账单 |
| `receipt_bill` | saas、ai经营分析收款账单 |
| `receipt_bill_detail` | 查询账单明细 |
| `receipt_bill_pay_trans` | 查询账单支付交易表 |
| `receipt_bill_pay_trans_detail` | 查询账单支付交易详情表 |
| `receipt_bill_refund_trans` | 账单退款表 |
| `receipt_bill_refund_trans_detail` | 账单退款明细表 |
| `merchant_message_config` | 经营快报 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE merchant_product (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_type smallint DEFAULT 1,
    product_code character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    product_id bigint NOT NULL,
    policy_type character varying(2) NOT NULL,
    pre_notice_date date,
    func_downgrade_date date,
    auto_pay_style character varying(64),
    auto_pay character varying(2) NOT NULL,
    renewal_status character varying(2) DEFAULT '0'::character varying,
    status character varying(2) DEFAULT '0'::character varying,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updatedby numeric(10,0),
    remark character varying(512),
    account_id numeric(12,0),
    enjoy_service_account_id numeric(12,0),
    experience_end_time timestamp without time zone,
    subscribe_start_time timestamp without time zone,
    service_end_time timestamp without time zone,
    subscribe_end_time timestamp without time zone,
    func_config jsonb,
    product_config jsonb,
    max_bill_end_time timestamp without time zone,
    extend_info jsonb
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.id is 'id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.merchant_id is '商家id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.merchant_type is '商户类型 0:运营商 1:代理商';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.product_code is '产品编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.product_id is '产品id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.policy_type is '计费规则类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.pre_notice_date is '提醒日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.func_downgrade_date is '功能降级日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.auto_pay_style is '自动扣款方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.auto_pay is '自动扣费:0:是 1:否';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.renewal_status is '续费状态 0:续费成功 1:续费失败 2:续费失败功能降级';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.status is '状态 0:使用中 1:自然终止 2:主动终止';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.createdby is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.updatedby is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.account_id is '订阅账号id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.enjoy_service_account_id is '享受服务账号id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.experience_end_time is '体验结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.subscribe_start_time is '订阅开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.service_end_time is '服务结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.subscribe_end_time is '订阅结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.func_config is '服务功能配置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.product_config is '产品配置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.max_bill_end_time is '最大账单账期结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_product.extend_info is '扩展信息，autoGray:自动转企业版，fallbackNormal:恢复普通版';
COMMENT ON TABLE merchant_product is '商家产品表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_merchant_product ON merchant_product USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_product_mp ON merchant_product USING btree (merchant_id, product_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_product_pnd ON merchant_product USING btree (pre_notice_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_product_rpi ON merchant_product USING btree (product_id);

CREATE TABLE receipt_bill (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_type smallint DEFAULT 1,
    charge_type character varying(2) DEFAULT '2'::character varying,
    pay_type character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    product_code character varying(64),
    status character varying(2) DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    close_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    settlement_completed_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    receivable_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    received_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    owed_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    refund_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    refund_status character varying(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    sort smallint DEFAULT 0,
    bill_payment_start_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updatedby numeric(10,0),
    account_id numeric(12,0),
    merchant_product_id numeric(12,0),
    marketing_config jsonb,
    func_config jsonb
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.id is '收款账单id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.merchant_type is '商户类型 0=运营商 1=代理商';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.charge_type is '收费类型 1:预付费,2:后付费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.pay_type is '支付方式 1:主动缴费,2:线下缴费,3:平台代扣,4:自动缴费,5:代理商缴费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.product_code is '产品编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.status is '状态 1:已出账 2:部分结清 3:已结清';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.close_time is '账单关闭时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.settlement_completed_time is '账单结清时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.receivable_amount is '应收金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.received_amount is '实收金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.owed_amount is '欠费金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.refund_amount is '退款金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.refund_status is '退款状态 1未退款 2部分退款 3已退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.sort is '扣款优先级，数值越小越优先';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.bill_payment_start_time is '账单缴费开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.createdby is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.updatedby is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.account_id is '账号id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.merchant_product_id is '商家产品记录id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.marketing_config is '营销配置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill.func_config is '服务功能配置';
COMMENT ON TABLE receipt_bill is '收款账单表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_receipt_bill ON receipt_bill USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_receipt_bill_mi ON receipt_bill USING btree (merchant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_receipt_bill_status ON receipt_bill USING btree (status);
CREATE INDEX idx_receipt_bill_ct_pt ON receipt_bill USING btree (charge_type, pay_type);
CREATE INDEX receipt_bill_merchant_product_id_idx ON receipt_bill USING btree (merchant_product_id);

CREATE TABLE receipt_bill_detail (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    receipt_bill_id bigint,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_type smallint DEFAULT 1,
    batch_no character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    product_id bigint,
    product_code character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    product_name character varying(64),
    product_charging_policy_id bigint,
    pay_type character varying(64),
    charge_type character varying(2) DEFAULT '2'::character varying,
    bill_start_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    bill_end_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    bill_close_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    bill_deadline timestamp(6) without time zone,
    original_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    receivable_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    received_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    owed_amount numeric(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    refund_amount numeric(10,2),
    bill_status character varying(2) DEFAULT '1'::character varying,
    description character varying(64),
    remark character varying(64),
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updatedby numeric(10,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.id is '账单明细详情id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.receipt_bill_id is '收款账单id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.merchant_type is '商户类型 0=运营商 1=代理商';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.batch_no is '批次号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.product_id is '产品id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.product_code is '产品code';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.product_name is '产品名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN receipt_bill_detail.product_charging_policy_id is '计费规则id';

```

```sql
-- 【merchant_product】saas、ai经营分析账单
select * from merchant_product where merchant_id =1094781 and product_code ='SAAS' order by created desc;

-- 【receipt_bill】saas、ai经营分析收款账单
select * from receipt_bill where merchant_id ='1095114' and product_code='SAAS' order by created desc

-- 【receipt_bill_detail】查询账单明细
select * from receipt_bill_detail where merchant_id ='1095114'and product_code='SAAS' order by created desc

-- 【receipt_bill_pay_trans】查询账单支付交易表
select * from receipt_bill_pay_trans where merchant_id ='1032498' order by created desc

-- 【receipt_bill_pay_trans_detail】查询账单支付交易详情表
select * from receipt_bill_pay_trans_detail where merchant_id ='1032498'  order by updated  desc

-- 【receipt_bill_refund_trans】账单退款表
select * from receipt_bill_refund_trans where merchant_id ='1033278' order by created desc

-- 【receipt_bill_refund_trans_detail】账单退款明细表
select * from receipt_bill_refund_trans_detail where merchant_id ='1033278'order by created desc

-- 【merchant_message_config】经营快报
select * from merchant_message_config where merchant_id ='1036030'
```

**库：`user_member`**（5 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `um_account_record` | 会员流水 |
| `um_account` | 总权益 |
| `um_account_benefit` | 权益明细 |
| `um_benefit_scope` | 权益范围 |
| `um_benefit` | 权益类型 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE um_account_record (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    account_id bigint NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    store_id numeric(12,0),
    equipment_id numeric(12,0),
    benefit_classify smallint NOT NULL,
    benefit_id bigint NOT NULL,
    initial_benefit numeric(10,2) NOT NULL,
    original_benefit numeric(10,2) NOT NULL,
    actual_benefit numeric(10,2) NOT NULL,
    out_trade_no character varying(64),
    order_no character varying(64),
    mode smallint NOT NULL,
    create_time timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    resource character varying(200),
    description character varying(200),
    trade_type smallint,
    trade_amount numeric(12,2),
    commodity_name character varying(64),
    store_name character varying(120),
    equipment_type_name character varying(16),
    equipment_value character varying(16),
    equipment_type_id numeric(12,0),
    record_type smallint,
    sort integer,
    account_benefit_id bigint,
    createdby numeric(10,0),
    create_name character varying(50),
    card_no character varying(50),
    refund_no character varying(64),
    goods_id numeric(10,0),
    goods_type smallint,
    goods_classify numeric(10,0),
    goods_classify_name character varying(20),
    goods_num numeric(6,0),
    discounts_amount numeric(12,2),
    operation_equipment_name character varying(32),
    equipment_name character varying(32),
    terminal_name character varying(32),
    merchant_benefit_classify_id numeric(10,0),
    merchant_benefit_classify_name character varying(32),
    operation_type smallint,
    operation_channel smallint,
    consume_type smallint,
    sub_order_no character varying(64),
    actual_value numeric(12,2),
    group_number numeric(10,0),
    goods_type_id bigint,
    goods_price numeric(12,2),
    goods_type_name character varying(64),
    account_initial_balance numeric(12,2),
    account_initial_num numeric(12,2)
)
PARTITION BY LIST (mod(merchant_id, (128)::numeric));
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.id is ' 流水号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.account_id is ' 账户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.user_id is '  平台用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.merchant_user_id is ' 商户用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.merchant_id is '  商户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.store_id is ' 店铺';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.equipment_id is ' 设备';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.benefit_classify is ' 权益类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.benefit_id is ' 权益ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.initial_benefit is '  期初权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.original_benefit is ' 权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.actual_benefit is ' 实际权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.out_trade_no is ' 交易单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.order_no is ' 业务单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.mode is ' 方式1=加，2=减';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.create_time is '  时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.resource is ' 来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.description is '  备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.trade_type is '交易类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.trade_amount is '交易金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.commodity_name is '商品名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.store_name is '场地/店铺名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.equipment_type_name is '设备类型名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.equipment_type_id is '设备类型id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.record_type is '流水自定义类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.sort is '消费使用排序';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.account_benefit_id is '账号权益明细id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.createdby is '操作人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.create_name is '操作人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.card_no is '卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.refund_no is '退兑换单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_id is '商品id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_type is '商品类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_classify is '商品所属分类';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_classify_name is '商品所属分类名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_num is '商品数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.discounts_amount is '优惠金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.operation_equipment_name is '操作设备名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.equipment_name is '设备名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.terminal_name is '终端名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.merchant_benefit_classify_id is '商家自定义权益id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.merchant_benefit_classify_name is '商家自定义权益名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.operation_type is '记录类型（1.商品购买记录 2.商品兑换记录；3.商品回收记录；4.储值变更记录（储值变更记录包括设备消费记录））';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.operation_channel is '操作渠道（1.微信小程序；2.桌面收银台；3.移动收银台）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.consume_type is '消费方式 （1.刷卡消费 2.微信小程序；3.桌面收银台；4.移动收银台）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.sub_order_no is '子单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.actual_value is '权益实际价值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.group_number is '机台号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_type_id is '商品类型id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_price is '商品单价';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.goods_type_name is '商品类型名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.account_initial_balance is '账号下权益初始总数（总次数）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_record.account_initial_num is '账号下频次卡权益初始总张数';
COMMENT ON TABLE um_account_record is '账户流水记录表';

CREATE TABLE um_account (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    total numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    balance numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    classify smallint NOT NULL,
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    description character varying(200),
    create_time timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    create_by numeric(12,0),
    update_time timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    update_by numeric(12,0),
    merchant_user_id numeric(19,0),
    card_no character varying(50),
    parent_account_id bigint,
    store_id bigint,
    down_time timestamp(3) without time zone,
    deposit numeric(6,2),
    default_flag boolean,
    frozen_amount numeric(10,2)
)
PARTITION BY LIST (mod(merchant_id, (128)::numeric));
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.id is '	账户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.user_id is '	用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.merchant_id is '	商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.total is '	总权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.balance is '	剩余权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.classify is '	权益类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.status is '状态：1=正常，2=禁用3=挂失，4=过期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.description is '	备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.create_time is '	创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.create_by is '创建人id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.update_time is '	更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.update_by is '	更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.merchant_user_id is '商家用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.card_no is '卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.parent_account_id is '父权益id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.store_id is '所属门店id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.down_time is '卡有效期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.deposit is '押金';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.default_flag is '是否是默认卡账户 true:是，false：否';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account.frozen_amount is '冻结金额';
COMMENT ON TABLE um_account is '会员账户表';

CREATE TABLE um_account_benefit (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    account_id bigint NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    benefit_id bigint NOT NULL,
    total numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    balance numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    classify smallint NOT NULL,
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    expiry_date_category smallint,
    up_time character varying(32),
    down_time character varying(32),
    create_time timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    create_by numeric(12,0),
    update_time timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    update_by numeric(12,0),
    resource smallint,
    resource_id bigint,
    merchant_id numeric NOT NULL,
    merchant_user_id numeric(19,0),
    merchant_benefit_classify_id numeric(10,0),
    use_rule_id bigint,
    store_id bigint,
    value_type smallint,
    frozen_amount numeric(10,2)
)
PARTITION BY LIST (mod(merchant_id, (128)::numeric));
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.id is '  标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.account_id is '  账户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.user_id is '用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.benefit_id is '  权益ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.total is ' 总权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.balance is ' 剩余权益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.classify is '  权益类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.status is '  状态：1=正常，2=过期， 3=已消耗';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.expiry_date_category is ' 有效期类型:0、无限期,1、日期区间可用,2、单日时间区间可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.up_time is ' 上线时间

如果是类型1(日期区间)，字段内容格式是:  yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss
如果是类型2(时间区间)，字段内容格式是:  HH:mm:ss';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.down_time is ' 下线时间

如果是类型1(日期区间)，字段内容格式是:  yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss
如果是类型2(时间区间)，字段内容格式是:  HH:mm:ss';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.create_time is ' 创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.create_by is '创建人id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.update_time is ' 更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.update_by is '更新人id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.resource is '权益来源类型：1=会员升级，2=权益规则触发';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.resource_id is '权益来源关联的业务id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.merchant_user_id is '商家用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.merchant_benefit_classify_id is '商家自定义权益id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.use_rule_id is '使用规则id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.store_id is '所属门店';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.value_type is '权益值类型，1：额度类型，2：频次类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_account_benefit.frozen_amount is '冻结金额';
COMMENT ON TABLE um_account_benefit is '会员账户明细表';

CREATE TABLE um_benefit_scope (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    benefit_id bigint,
    applicable smallint,
    associated_id numeric,
    create_time timestamp(0) without time zone,
    update_time timestamp(0) without time zone,
    merchant_id numeric
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.benefit_id is '权益ID
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.applicable is '适用类型 1 品类 2 场地 3 设备 4 商品';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.associated_id is '关联ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit_scope.merchant_id is '商户Id';
COMMENT ON TABLE um_benefit_scope is '权益范围表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_um_benfit_scope ON um_benefit_scope USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX um_benefit_scope_merchant_benefit_idx ON um_benefit_scope USING btree (merchant_id, benefit_id);
CREATE INDEX um_benefit_scope_associated_applicable_idx ON um_benefit_scope USING btree (associated_id, applicable);
CREATE INDEX um_benefit_scope_benefit_idx ON um_benefit_scope USING hash (benefit_id);

CREATE TABLE um_benefit (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric,
    title character varying(32),
    sub_title character varying(32),
    code character varying(32),
    classify smallint,
    benefit_count numeric,
    expiry_date_category smallint NOT NULL,
    up_time character varying(32),
    down_time character varying(32),
    show_date_category smallint,
    show_up_time character varying(32),
    show_down_time character varying(32),
    is_active boolean,
    create_time timestamp(0) without time zone,
    update_time timestamp(0) without time zone
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.merchant_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.title is '标题
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.sub_title is '副标题';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.code is '权益编码
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.classify is '权益分类
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.benefit_count is '权益数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.expiry_date_category is '可用有效期类型 0、无限期 1、日期区间可用 2、单日时间区间可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.up_time is '上线时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.down_time is '下线时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.show_date_category is '可见有效期类型
0、无限期  1、日期区间可用 2、单日时间区间可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.show_up_time is '可见上线时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.show_down_time is '可见下线时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.is_active is '是否可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN um_benefit.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON TABLE um_benefit is '权益表';
CREATE INDEX um_benefit_merchant_classify__idx ON um_benefit USING btree (merchant_id, classify, up_time, down_time);
CREATE INDEX um_benefit_merchant_classify_expiry_date_category_active_idx ON um_benefit USING btree (merchant_id, classify, expiry_date_category, is_active);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_um_benfit ON um_benefit USING btree (id);
```

```sql
-- 【um_account_record】会员流水
select * from um_account_record uar  where uar.merchant_id  = '1035814' and uar.user_id  = '2200009773' order by uar.create_time desc;

-- 【um_account】总权益
select * from um_account where user_id='2200007861' and merchant_id=1035814 order by update_time desc ;

-- 【um_account_benefit】权益明细
select * from um_account_benefit where user_id='2200009773'and merchant_id=1035814  order by update_time  desc ;

-- 【um_benefit_scope】权益范围
select * from lyy.um_benefit_scope where benefit_id ='1152274125952294912'

-- 【um_benefit】权益类型
select * from lyy.um_benefit where merchant_id ='1035814' order by create_time desc
```

**库：`selectdb_payment`**（5 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `dwd_payment_payment_record_trade` | 查询每个订单在移动端报表内实际增加和退款金额 |
| `dwd_payment_refund_record_trade` | 查询退款交易 |
| `ads_order_info_agg` | 查询订单信息 |
| `ods_unified_redeem_order` | 经营统计查抖音核销数据、经营统计查美团核销数据 |
| `ads_payment_equipment_record_di` | 经营统计每日的收入概览、启动概览、储值概览、每日经营趋势、收款金额趋势、时段经营趋势、收款分析 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE `dwd_payment_payment_record_trade` (
  `out_trade_no` varchar(500) NULL,
  `order_no` varchar(500) NULL,
  `ds` date NULL,
  `distributor_id` bigint NULL,

  `amount` decimal(11,2) NULL,
  `confirm_date` date NULL,
  `pay_amount` decimal(16,2) NULL,
  `merchant_code` varchar(500) NULL,
  `channel_code` int NULL,
  `settlement_channel_code` int NULL,
  `open_id` varchar(500) NULL,
  `app_id` varchar(500) NULL,
  `channel_order_no` varchar(500) NULL,
  `third_order_no` varchar(500) NULL,
  `status` tinyint NULL,
  `refund_mark` tinyint NULL,

  `f_reserve` bigint NULL,
  `create_by` bigint NULL,
  `update_time` datetime(3) NULL,
  `update_by` bigint NULL,
  `organization_id` bigint NULL,
  `pay_method` varchar(500) NULL,
  `pay_method_name` varchar(500) NULL,
  `channel_merchant_order_no` varchar(500) NULL,
  `certificate` json NULL,
  `attach` text NULL,
  `out_app_id` varchar(500) NULL,
  `pay_ad_org_id` bigint NULL,
  `pay_merchant_code` varchar(500) NULL,
  `use_pay_merchant_code_biz_type` tinyint NULL,
  `settlement_amount` decimal(16,2) NULL,
  `settlement_status` tinyint NULL,
  `product_code2` varchar(500) NULL,
  `terminal_no` varchar(500) NULL,
  `product_code` varchar(500) NULL,
  `pay_service_fee` decimal(10,2) NULL,
  `merchant_out_app_id` varchar(500) NULL,
  `deduction_payment_service_fee` decimal(10,2) NULL,
  `failed_description` text NULL,
  `has_service_fee_deduction` tinyint NULL,
  `deduction_merchant_code` varchar(500) NULL,
  `settlement_date` date NULL,
  `order_amount` tinyint NULL,

  `province_id` bigint NULL,
  `city_id` bigint NULL,
  `province` varchar(500) NULL,
  `city` varchar(500) NULL,
  `district` varchar(500) NULL,
  `equipment_id` bigint NULL,
  `e_code` varchar(40) NULL,
  `e_type_id` bigint NULL,
  `e_type_value` varchar(500) NULL,
  `e_type_name` varchar(500) NULL,
  `store_id` bigint NULL,
  `e_store_name` varchar(500) NULL,
  `merchant_name` varchar(500) NULL,
  `merchant_short_name` varchar(1024) NULL,
  `id_code` varchar(500) NULL,
  `business_license` varchar(500) NULL,
  `account_code` varchar(500) NULL,
  `account_name` varchar(500) NULL,
  `merchant_type` char(1) NULL,
  `account_type` char(1) NULL,
  `bank_short_name` varchar(1024) NULL,
  `bank_name` varchar(1024) NULL,
  `hour` tinyint NULL,
  `receivable_amount` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `received_amount` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `platform_tying_amount` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `platform_tying_service_fee` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `platform_tying_deduction_divide_service_fee` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `platform_amount` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `platform_service_fee` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `platform_deduction_divide_service_fee` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `divide_amount` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `divide_service_fee` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `deduction_divide_service_fee` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `independent_amount` decimal(38,2) NULL,
  `pay_amount_level` text NULL,
  `pay_type` text NULL,
  `month` date NULL,
  `year` date NULL,
  `is_workday` text NULL,
  `is_weekday` text NULL,
  `is_week_year` bigint NULL,
  `week_year_rnk` bigint NULL,
  `week_day` bigint NULL,
  `week_sta` date NULL,
  `week_end` date NULL,

  `card_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "商户卡ID",

  `primary_clearing_category_code` varchar(64) NULL COMMENT "清分一级类目",
  `second_clearing_category_code` varchar(64) NULL COMMENT "清分二级类目",
  `third_clearing_category_code` varchar(64) NULL COMMENT "清分三级类目",
  `trans_category` smallint NULL COMMENT "交易类型聚合 1启动 2充值"
) ENGINE=OLAP
UNIQUE KEY(`out_trade_no`, `order_no`, `ds`)
PARTITION BY RANGE(`ds`)()
DISTRIBUTED BY HASH(`out_trade_no`) BUCKETS 8
PROPERTIES (

CREATE TABLE `ods_unified_redeem_order` (
  `source_table` varchar(64) NOT NULL COMMENT "来源表名(third_redeem_record,meituan_redeem_record,tiktok_activity_order)",
  `source_id` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT "来源表主键ID",
  `day` date NOT NULL COMMENT "日期分区字段(来源于created)",
  `channel` varchar(32) NOT NULL COMMENT "渠道: meituan/tiktok",
  `business_type` varchar(32) NOT NULL COMMENT "业务类型: self/assembly",
  `distributor_id` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT "商户ID",
  `equipment_group_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "场地ID",
  `equipment_type_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "设备类型ID",
  `equipment_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "设备ID",
  `user_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "用户ID",
  `position_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "仓位ID",
  `locate` bigint NULL COMMENT "仓位编号",
  `province_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "省份ID",
  `city_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "城市ID",
  `district_id` bigint NULL COMMENT "区县ID",
  `code` varchar(255) NULL COMMENT "券码",
  `title` varchar(255) NULL COMMENT "商品名称",
  `redeem_amount` decimal(10,2) NULL COMMENT "核销金额",
  `goods_amount` decimal(10,2) NULL COMMENT "商品金额",
  `pay_amount` decimal(10,2) NULL COMMENT "支付金额",
  `redeem_type` smallint NULL COMMENT "核销类型",
  `status` smallint NULL COMMENT "统一状态: 1=初始化 2=已核销 3=已退款 4=失败",
  `good_info` json NULL COMMENT "商品信息JSON",
  `ext_data` json NULL COMMENT "渠道特有字段JSON",
  `created` datetime NOT NULL COMMENT "创建时间",
  `updated` datetime NULL COMMENT "更新时间"
) ENGINE=OLAP
UNIQUE KEY(`source_table`, `source_id`, `day`)
PARTITION BY RANGE(`day`)()
DISTRIBUTED BY HASH(`source_table`, `source_id`) BUCKETS 2
PROPERTIES (
"file_cache_ttl_seconds" = "0",
"is_being_synced" = "false",
"dynamic_partition.enable" = "true",
"dynamic_partition.time_unit" = "DAY",
"dynamic_partition.time_zone" = "Asia/Shanghai",
"dynamic_partition.start" = "-1460",
"dynamic_partition.end" = "3",
"dynamic_partition.prefix" = "p",
"dynamic_partition.buckets" = "2",
"dynamic_partition.create_history_partition" = "true",
"dynamic_partition.history_partition_num" = "-1",
"dynamic_partition.hot_partition_num" = "0",
"dynamic_partition.reserved_history_periods" = "NULL",
"storage_medium" = "hdd",
"storage_format" = "V2",
"inverted_index_storage_format" = "V2",
"enable_unique_key_merge_on_write" = "true",
"light_schema_change" = "true",
"disable_auto_compaction" = "false",
"enable_single_replica_compaction" = "false",
"group_commit_interval_ms" = "10000",
"group_commit_data_bytes" = "134217728",
"enable_mow_light_delete" = "false"
);

PARTITION BY RANGE(`ds`)()
DISTRIBUTED BY HASH(`distributor_id`) BUCKETS 8
PROPERTIES (
"file_cache_ttl_seconds" = "0",
"is_being_synced" = "false",
"dynamic_partition.enable" = "true",
"dynamic_partition.time_unit" = "DAY",
"dynamic_partition.time_zone" = "Asia/Shanghai",
"dynamic_partition.start" = "-2147483648",
"dynamic_partition.end" = "3",
"dynamic_partition.prefix" = "p",
"dynamic_partition.buckets" = "8",
"dynamic_partition.create_history_partition" = "false",
"dynamic_partition.history_partition_num" = "1460",
"dynamic_partition.hot_partition_num" = "0",
"dynamic_partition.reserved_history_periods" = "NULL",
"storage_medium" = "hdd",
"storage_format" = "V2",
"inverted_index_storage_format" = "V2",
"enable_unique_key_merge_on_write" = "true",
"light_schema_change" = "true",
"disable_auto_compaction" = "false",
"enable_single_replica_compaction" = "false",
"group_commit_interval_ms" = "10000",
"group_commit_data_bytes" = "134217728",
"enable_mow_light_delete" = "false"
);
```

```sql
-- 【dwd_payment_payment_record_trade】查询每个订单在移动端报表内实际增加和退款金额
select aa.out_trade_no, aa.amount, aa.receivable_amount,aa.store_id ,aa.e_store_name,aa.e_code,aa.create_time ,aa.trade_time,
       dprrt.refund_amount, dprrt.receivable_refund_amount
from dwd_payment_payment_record_trade aa
left join (
    select out_trade_no, sum(refund_amount) as refund_amount,
           sum(receivable_refund_amount) as receivable_refund_amount
    from dwd_payment_refund_record_trade
    where (ds>='2026-05-22' and ds<='2026-05-22')
     and status not in ('3','4')
    group by out_trade_no
) dprrt on aa.out_trade_no =dprrt.out_trade_no
where (aa.ds>='2026-05-22' and aa.ds<='2026-05-22')
 and aa.distributor_id ='1073579'
  -- and aa.store_id ='1092037'
   --and aa.e_code ='77289630'
-- and aa.e_store_name ='no分账'
-- and aa.out_trade_no ='PA103026012915421922000124151436'
order by aa.create_time desc;

-- 【dwd_payment_refund_record_trade】查询退款交易
select * from dwd_payment_refund_record_trade where ds='2026-02-03' and distributor_id = '1399163' and e_code ='83918187'

-- 【ads_order_info_agg】查询订单信息
select * from ads_order_info_agg where  out_trade_no ='PA101525082813502922000055181532' and ds='2025-08-28'

-- 【ods_unified_redeem_order】经营统计查抖音核销数据
select * from ods_unified_redeem_order where distributor_id ='1073579' and `day` ='2026-07-16'

-- 【ods_unified_redeem_order】经营统计查美团核销数据
select * from ods_unified_redeem_order where distributor_id ='1073579' and `day` ='2026-07-17'

-- 【ads_payment_equipment_record_di】经营统计每日的收入概览、启动概览、储值概览、每日经营趋势、收款金额趋势、时段经营趋势、收款分析
select * from ads_payment_equipment_record_di aperd where ds='2026-07-24' and distributor_id ='1399163' and `type` ='商家维度'
```

**库：`cmember`**（3 张表）

| `wash_product_order` | 查洗水商创产品 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE wash_dimension_user_info (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT nextval('wash_dimension_user_info_seq'::regclass),
    user_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    activity_info jsonb,
    created_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    deleted boolean DEFAULT false,
    created_by numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated_by numeric(10,0) NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.id is 'ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.user_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.activity_info is '活动相关信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.created_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.updated_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.deleted is 'true表示已删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_dimension_user_info.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON TABLE wash_dimension_user_info is '洗水用户维度信息表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_wash_dimension_user_info ON wash_dimension_user_info USING btree (id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_wash_dimension_user_info_user_id ON wash_dimension_user_info USING btree (user_id);

CREATE TABLE lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT nextval('wash_activity_rule_equipment_seq'::regclass),
    merchant_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_value character varying(40) NOT NULL,
    product_type smallint NOT NULL,
    scene smallint NOT NULL,
    type smallint NOT NULL,
    remark character varying(32),
    created_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    deleted boolean DEFAULT false,
    created_by numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated_by numeric(10,0) NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.id is 'ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.merchant_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.product_type is '洗水活动类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.scene is '场景码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.type is '0白名单 1黑名单';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.created_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.updated_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.deleted is 'true表示已删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON TABLE lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment is '洗水活动规则-设备名单';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_wash_activity_rule_equipment ON lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_wash_activity_rule_equipment_merchant_id_equipment_value ON lyy.wash_activity_rule_equipment USING btree (merchant_id, equipment_value, type, deleted);

CREATE TABLE promotion_activity_merchant (
    promotion_activity_merchant_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_name character varying(128),
    merchant_phone character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    activity_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    activity_type integer NOT NULL,
    is_unique smallint NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(3) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(3) without time zone,
    createdby numeric(10,0),
    updatedby numeric(10,0),
    createdby_name character varying(64),
    merchant_config jsonb,
    status smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.merchant_name is '商户名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.merchant_phone is '商户账号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.activity_id is '活动id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.activity_type is '活动类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.is_unique is '是否互斥1-是，2-否';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.merchant_config is '商户配置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_merchant.status is 'null或0表示开启   1关闭状态';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX promotion_activity_merchant_pkey ON promotion_activity_merchant USING btree (promotion_activity_merchant_id);
CREATE INDEX promotion_activity_merchant_activity_idx ON promotion_activity_merchant USING btree (activity_id, merchant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_promotion_activity_merchant_mid_atype ON promotion_activity_merchant USING btree (merchant_id, activity_type);

CREATE TABLE wash_product_order (
    id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    product_type smallint NOT NULL,
    out_trade_no character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    order_source character varying(30) NOT NULL,
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    user_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_value character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    store_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    pay_detail jsonb NOT NULL,
    equipment_detail jsonb,
    product_detail jsonb NOT NULL,
    refund_time timestamp without time zone,
    refund_amount numeric(11,2),
    created_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    deleted boolean DEFAULT false,
    created_by numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated_by numeric(10,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.product_type is '产品类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.out_trade_no is '订单编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.order_source is '订单来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.status is '订单状态  1 已支付 2 已退款 ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.merchant_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.store_id is '场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.pay_detail is '支付详情';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.equipment_detail is '设备详情';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.product_detail is '产品详情';
COMMENT ON COLUMN wash_product_order.refund_time is '退款时间';
COMMENT ON TABLE wash_product_order is '洗水产品订单表';
CREATE INDEX wash_product_order_userid ON wash_product_order USING btree (user_id, status, product_type, created_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_wash_product_order_psctime ON wash_product_order USING btree (product_type, status, created_time DESC);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX wash_product_order_pk ON wash_product_order USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_wash_product_order_oid ON wash_product_order USING btree (out_trade_no);
```

```sql
-- 【wash_dimension_user_info】查洗水商创活动退款、投诉记录
select * from wash_dimension_user_info

-- 【promotion_activity_merchant】查洗水商家维度商创活动
SELECT id, merchant_id, equipment_value, product_type, scene, type, remark, created_time, updated_time, deleted, created_by, updated_by FROM wash_activity_rule_equipment 
WHERE 1=1 AND merchant_id IN ( 1036016 ) AND product_type IN ( 4 ) AND type = 1 and deleted = false ORDER BY updated_time DESC LIMIT 10

select * from wash_product_order
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE merchant_equipment_board (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0),
    equipment_id numeric(12,0),
    parent_equipment_id numeric(12,0) DEFAULT 0,
    store_id numeric(12,0),
    store_name character varying(255),
    group_number numeric(12,0),
    physical_tag_ids numeric(12,0)[],
    unique_code character varying(32),
    equipment_value character varying(40),
    equipment_status smallint,
    equipment_work_status smallint,
    description character varying(255),
    offline_time timestamp without time zone,
    signal numeric(10,0),
    equipment_type_id numeric(12,0),
    equipment_type_value character varying(20),
    equipment_type_name character varying(40),
    factory_brand character varying(30),
    remarks character varying(255),
    created timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    created_by numeric(12,0) DEFAULT 0,
    updated_by numeric(12,0),
    is_active boolean,
    status character varying(100),
    login_flag numeric(8,0),
    business_type numeric(8,0),
    channel_id character varying(20),
    skb_connect_status numeric(8,0),
    expire_at numeric(16,0) DEFAULT 0,
    related_status smallint,
    related_device_type smallint,
    subsidy_status smallint,
    health_status smallint,
    agent_user_id bigint,
    current_agent_user_id bigint,
    door_lock_state smallint,
    human_presence smallint
) WITH (fillfactor=100, autovacuum_vacuum_threshold=2000, autovacuum_vacuum_scale_factor=0.5);
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.id is '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.merchant_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.parent_equipment_id is '上级设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.store_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.store_name is '场地名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.group_number is '场地内编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.physical_tag_ids is '物理标签';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.unique_code is '设备唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_status is '设备状态 1=在线   0=离线';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_work_status is '设备工作状态    1=工作中  2= 空闲    0=故障';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.description is '离线原因/故障原因';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.offline_time is '离线时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.signal is '信号值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_type_id is '设备类型id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_type_value is '设备类型值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.equipment_type_name is '设备类型名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.factory_brand is '工厂品牌';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.remarks is '设备备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.created is '创建日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.updated is '更新日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.is_active is '是否可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.status is ' 设备状态(启用:非disabled字段均可，默认使用1Normal   禁用:disabled)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.login_flag is '登录标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.business_type is '业务类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.channel_id is '通道ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.skb_connect_status is '刷卡版链接状态  0=无链接 1=通讯正常';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.expire_at is '会话时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.related_status is '关联的加液机状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.related_device_type is '关联的加液机类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.subsidy_status is '补贴状态(0=不补贴,1=补贴 目前为支付宝补贴设备使用）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.health_status is '健康检测连接状态(null=无连接 1=通讯正常)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.door_lock_state is '门锁状态(0=关闭 1=开启 NULL=从未上报，来源：iot_equipment_extension 经 device-event-hub 同步)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merchant_equipment_board.human_presence is '人体感应(0=无人 1=有人 NULL=从未上报，来源同上)';
COMMENT ON TABLE merchant_equipment_board is '设备IOT宽表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_lyy_merchant_equipment_board_new ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_board_new_evalue_pequipment_id ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (parent_equipment_id, equipment_value);
CREATE INDEX idx_merchant_equipment_board_new_mid_ev ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (merchant_id, equipment_value, equipment_type_id);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_board_new_equipment_id_idx ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (equipment_id);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_board_new_equipment_value_idx ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (equipment_value);
CREATE INDEX merchant_equipment_board_new_store_id_idx ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (store_id) WHERE (store_id IS NOT NULL);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX merchant_equipment_board_new_unique_code_idx ON merchant_equipment_board USING btree (unique_code);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE ticket (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    code character varying(50),
    title character varying(50),
    category_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    group_id numeric(12,0),
    status smallint,
    timeout_limit timestamp(6) without time zone,
    processor_id numeric(12,0),
    processor_group_id numeric(12,0),
    create_by_source smallint,
    is_active boolean DEFAULT true,
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    update_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    create_by numeric(12,0),
    update_by numeric(12,0),
    close_timeout_limit timestamp(6) without time zone,
    reply_timeout_limit timestamp(6) without time zone,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    participants numeric(12,0)[],
    description character varying(255),
    business_system smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.code is '工单编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.title is '工单标题';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.category_id is '工单分类id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.group_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.status is '工单状态：0-初始化，1-待处理，2-处理中，3-已关闭';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.timeout_limit is '超时截止时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.processor_id is '当前处理人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.processor_group_id is '当前处理组';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.create_by_source is '创建人来源：0-系统用户，1-C端用户，2-B端用户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.is_active is '设备类型编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.create_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.update_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.close_timeout_limit is '关闭超时时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.reply_timeout_limit is '自动回复超时时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.merchant_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.participants is '商家参与者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.description is '工单描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ticket.business_system is '业务系统(null或者1 代表乐摇摇默认的, 2代表多金宝)';
COMMENT ON TABLE ticket is '工单表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ticket_pk ON ticket USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ticket_merchant_id_create_time ON ticket USING btree (merchant_id, create_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_ticket_merchant_id_group_id_create_time ON ticket USING btree (group_id, merchant_id, create_time);
```

**库：`massage_chair`**（3 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE lyy_ic_card (
    lyy_ic_card_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    merchant_id numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    factory_id numeric(11,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    card_no character varying(32) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    amount numeric(8,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    modify_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    memo character varying(256) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    user_name character varying(64),
    phone numeric(11,0),
    status smallint DEFAULT 1,
    type smallint DEFAULT 0,
    user_id numeric(12,0),
    lyy_group_id numeric(11,0),
    school_id numeric(10,0),
    class_id numeric(10,0),
    sub_type smallint,
    contact_name character varying(32),
    charge_mode character varying(64),
    output_water_type character varying(64),
    single_water_amount numeric(6,0),
    water_work_mode smallint,
    bind_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    expiration_date date,
    present numeric(8,2),
    remark character varying(32),
    third_user_id character varying(32),
    third_platform character varying(16) DEFAULT '0'::character varying,
    grade_id numeric(10,0),
    limit_status smallint,
    limit_type smallint,
    prestore_status numeric(4,0),
    support_member_card smallint,
    charging_count_limit smallint,
    day_limit_amount numeric(11,2),
    month_limit_amount numeric(11,2)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.lyy_ic_card_id is '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.merchant_id is 'ad_org表的用户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.factory_id is '工厂ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.card_no is '卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.amount is '卡余额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.modify_time is '修改时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.memo is '说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.user_name is '用户姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.phone is '手机号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.status is '卡状态：0-禁用，1-正常，2-挂失，3-未绑定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.type is ' 卡类型，默认为0 0-离线卡，1-在线卡';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.lyy_group_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.school_id is '学校ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.class_id is '班级ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.sub_type is '1为水杯，2为ic卡';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.contact_name is '联系人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.charge_mode is '计费方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.output_water_type is '出水方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.single_water_amount is '单次取水量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.water_work_mode is '售水机工作方式(1 在线 2离线)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.bind_time is '绑定时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.expiration_date is '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.present is '赠送金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.third_user_id is '第三方平台ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.third_platform is '第三方平台枚举 0:乐摇摇 DJ:东晙';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.grade_id is '年级ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.limit_status is '限制状态 0=未限制 1=已限额 2=手动解除限额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.limit_type is '限制类型 0= 日限额 1= 月限额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.prestore_status is '预存状态:0待领取,1已领取';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.support_member_card is '支持会员卡类型：0或null=不支持，1=次卡';

COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card.month_limit_amount is '月使用限制金额';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX lyy_ic_card_pkey ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (lyy_ic_card_id);
CREATE INDEX lyy_ic_card_index_user_name ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (user_name);
CREATE INDEX fk_index_merchant_id_card_no ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (merchant_id, card_no);
CREATE INDEX idx_lyy_ic_card_uid_mid ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (user_id, merchant_id);
CREATE INDEX lyy_ic_card_index_phone ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (phone);
CREATE INDEX lyy_ic_card_index_card_no ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (card_no);
CREATE INDEX lyy_ic_card_user_idx ON lyy_ic_card USING hash (user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lyy_ic_card_school_id ON lyy_ic_card USING btree (school_id);

CREATE TABLE lyy_ic_card_flow (
    lyy_ic_card_flow_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_no character(16),
    lyy_user_id numeric(11,0),
    merchant_id numeric(11,0),
    card_no character varying(32),
    flow_amount numeric(8,2),
    flow_type numeric(2,0) NOT NULL,
    ext character varying(64),
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    lyy_equipment_group character varying(50),
    memo character varying(64),
    status numeric(1,0),
    amount numeric(8,2),
    receive_status numeric(1,0),
    refund_amount numeric(8,2),
    refund_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    expiration_date date,
    time_limit bigint,
    usage_amount numeric(8,0),
    charge_mode character varying(64),
    order_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_ic_card_id numeric(11,0),
    present numeric(8,2),
    flow_present numeric(8,2),
    passway numeric(4,0),
    start_param character varying(1024),
    deduction_remarks character varying(64),
    actual_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    order_no character varying(64),
    update_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    refund_present numeric(8,2)
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (create_time);
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_ic_card_flow_id is '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.equipment_no is '设备号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.merchant_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.card_no is '卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.flow_amount is '流水记录金额，单位：元';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.flow_type is ' 1扫码充卡 2刷卡充电 3使用账户余额充卡 4回刷停止充电 5充电自动停止 6-扫码充卡结果 7-打卡洗衣 8-刷卡洗衣启动失败 9-剩余费用退款,10 实际消费金额 ,11 刷卡取水,12手工退款 13销卡,14余额转入,15商家赠送,16销卡退款,17商家修改增加余额,18商家修改减少余额, 19消费扣款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.ext is '扩展字段和流水类型匹配，对应充值订单号、补卡的卡号
';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_equipment_group is '设备归属的场地名称可改变';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.memo is '说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.status is '0初始状态 
1操作成功  
2失败 
3超时 
4无效 -1已退款';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.amount is 'ic卡当前的余额，计算后的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.receive_status is '圈存版充值流水领取状态：0-待领取；1-领取中；2-已领取';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.refund_amount is '充值余额-退款金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.refund_time is '退款时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.expiration_date is '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.time_limit is '有效期（天）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.usage_amount is '使用量(售水机为出水量 ml)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.charge_mode is '计费方式（TIME_INTERVAL-按时段计费，TIMES-按次数计费）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.order_id is '业务订单Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.lyy_ic_card_id is 'ic卡ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.present is 'ic卡当前的赠送余额，计算后的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.flow_present is '赠送金额流水';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.passway is '端口';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.start_param is '启动参数(参考:LifeOrderStartParamDTO)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.deduction_remarks is '扣款备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.actual_time is '消费时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.order_no is '中台支付单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN lyy_ic_card_flow.refund_present is '赠送余额-退款金额';
COMMENT ON TABLE lyy_ic_card_flow is '卡流水金额';

CREATE TABLE ic_card_limit_config (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    store_id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    day_limit_amount numeric(11,2),
    month_limit_amount numeric(11,2),
    create_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,

    create_by numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,

);

COMMENT ON COLUMN ic_card_limit_config.store_id is '场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ic_card_limit_config.day_limit_amount is '日限制使用金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ic_card_limit_config.month_limit_amount is '日限制使用金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ic_card_limit_config.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ic_card_limit_config.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN ic_card_limit_config.create_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON TABLE ic_card_limit_config is 'ic卡使用限制配置表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ic_card_limit_config_pk ON ic_card_limit_config USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX ic_card_limit_config_store_id ON ic_card_limit_config USING btree (store_id);
```

**库：`lyy_infor`**（5 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `xiaole_user_points_record` | 查app用户积分记录 |
| `xiaole_life_user` | 查app用户信息 |
| `xiaole_life_user_relate` | 查app用户与lyy、ls系统用户关联关系 |
| `xiaole_black_user` | 查不跳转app用户黑名单 |
| `xiaole_user_no_app_request` | 查app不使用申请黑名单 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE xiaole_user_points_record (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    record_type smallint NOT NULL,
    p_from smallint,
    from_id bigint,
    p_to smallint,
    to_id bigint,
    points numeric NOT NULL,
    valid smallint NOT NULL,
    create_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    update_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    deleted boolean NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.id is '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.user_id is '用户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.record_type is '记录类型，获取、消耗';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.p_from is '积分来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.from_id is '如果from是PointsTask，就拿具体的task，签到的话，这个fromId就为空';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.p_to is '积分消耗处';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.to_id is '如果to是exchange，就拿具体的PointsCommodity的id，如果是过期的话，这个toId就为空';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.points is '积分值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.valid is '这条记录拿到的积分是否还有效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_points_record.deleted is '是否删除';
COMMENT ON TABLE xiaole_user_points_record is '用户获取与消耗积分记录表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX xiaole_user_points_record_pkey ON xiaole_user_points_record USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_points_userid ON xiaole_user_points_record USING btree (user_id);

CREATE TABLE xiaole_life_user (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    telephone character varying(20),
    nickname character varying(64),
    head_img character varying(255),
    gender smallint NOT NULL,
    birthday timestamp without time zone,
    status smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    last_cancel_time timestamp without time zone,
    total_points numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    app_version character varying(64),
    app_endpoint character varying(20),
    mobile_model character varying(32),
    create_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    update_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    deleted boolean NOT NULL,
    sign_notify smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    device_id character varying(64),
    password character varying(64),
    source smallint,
    app_name character varying(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'xiaolelife'::character varying
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.id is 'ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.telephone is '手机号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.nickname is '昵称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.head_img is '头像';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.gender is ' 性别 1-男 2-女 0-未知';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.birthday is '生日';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.status is '用户状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.last_cancel_time is '上次注销时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.total_points is '用户总积分';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.app_version is '应用版本';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.app_endpoint is '应用终端';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.mobile_model is '移动端型号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.deleted is '是否删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.sign_notify is '签到通知';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.device_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.password is '登录密码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.source is '用户来源，1、微信，2、支付宝';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user.app_name is '应用名称';
COMMENT ON TABLE xiaole_life_user is '小乐生活APP用户表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX xiaole_life_user_pkey ON xiaole_life_user USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_telephone ON xiaole_life_user USING btree (telephone);
CREATE INDEX idx_xiaole_life_user_user_telephone ON xiaole_life_user USING btree (telephone);

CREATE TABLE xiaole_life_user_relate (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    subject_platform character varying(20),
    subject_app_id character varying(64),
    subject_app_name character varying(64),
    subject_app_secret character varying(64),
    main_user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    open_id character varying(64),
    open_user_avatar character varying(255),
    platform_user_id character varying(64),
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    create_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    update_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    deleted boolean NOT NULL,
    client_type character varying(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'wechat'::character varying,
    mark_singled boolean DEFAULT false,
    is_default boolean,
    open_nickname character varying(100)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.id is 'ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.subject_platform is '接入主体所在平台:微信、支付宝、抖音等';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.subject_app_id is '接入主体应用id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.subject_app_name is '接入主体应用名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.subject_app_secret is '接入主体应用密钥';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.main_user_id is '小乐应用主平台Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.open_id is '开放平台用户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.open_user_avatar is '开放平台用户头像';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.platform_user_id is '关联的乐摇摇体系下的用户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.status is '记录状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.create_time is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.update_time is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.updated_by is '更新人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.deleted is '是否删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.client_type is '客户端,wechat,alipay';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.mark_singled is '是否已标记单通道';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.is_default is '是否默认记录';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_life_user_relate.open_nickname is '第三方昵称';
COMMENT ON TABLE xiaole_life_user_relate is '小乐用户关联平台用户记录表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX xiaole_life_user_relate_pkey ON xiaole_life_user_relate USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX idx_xiaole_life_user_relate_pdctime ON xiaole_life_user_relate USING btree (platform_user_id, deleted, create_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_relate_platform_id ON xiaole_life_user_relate USING btree (platform_user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_relate_multi_id ON xiaole_life_user_relate USING btree (main_user_id, platform_user_id, subject_platform);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_relate_user ON xiaole_life_user_relate USING btree (main_user_id, subject_platform);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_relate_platform_user ON xiaole_life_user_relate USING btree (platform_user_id, subject_platform);

CREATE TABLE xiaole_black_user (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    black_type integer NOT NULL,
    telephone character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    create_time timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by character varying(20) NOT NULL,

    deleted boolean NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_black_user.black_type is '黑名单类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_black_user.telephone is '手机号';
COMMENT ON TABLE xiaole_black_user is '小乐生活黑名单用户';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX xiaole_black_user_pkey ON xiaole_black_user USING btree (id);

CREATE TABLE xiaole_user_no_app_request (
    id bigint NOT NULL,

COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_no_app_request.telephone is '手机号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_no_app_request.reason is '提交原因';
COMMENT ON COLUMN xiaole_user_no_app_request.status is '审核状态，0：未审核，1：已审核';
COMMENT ON TABLE xiaole_user_no_app_request is '用户提交不要app的申请';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX xiaole_user_no_app_request_pkey ON xiaole_user_no_app_request USING btree (id);
```
