**库：`order`**（2 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `order_info` | 查订单信息 |
| `refund_order_info` | 查订单退款信息 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE `order_info` (
  `out_trade_no` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL COMMENT '交易订单号',
  `parent_no` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '父订单号',
  `order_type` tinyint(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '订单类型 1:普通订单 2:虚拟单',
  `user_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '用户id',
  `distributor_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '商户id',
  `store_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '店铺id',
  `order_amount` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '订单金额',
  `actual_amount` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实际金额',
  `marketing_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '营销详情',
  `status` tinyint(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '订单状态 1:待支付 2:已支付 3:退款中 4:已退款 5:已取消 6:未支付 7:已关闭',
  `trans_type` tinyint(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '交易类型',
  `business_type` smallint(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务编号',
  `create_time` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP COMMENT '订单创建时间',
  `goods_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '商品详情',
  `equipment_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '设备详情',
  `discount_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '优惠详情',
  `member_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '会员详情',
  `pay_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '支付详情',
  `client_type` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户端类型',
  `pay_order_no` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '支付单号',
  `sub_order_num` int(2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '子单数量',
  `id` bigint(11) NOT NULL auto_increment,
  `remark` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '订单备注',
  `operator` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作人信息',
  `paid_amount` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL DEFAULT '0.00' COMMENT '已付金额',
  `update_time` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `create_by` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人',
  `update_by` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改人',
  `organization_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '组织id',
  `sales_order_no` varchar(50) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '销售单号',
  `extend_str` text CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务扩展字段',
  `common_extend` text CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '通用扩展字段',
  `refund_mark` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款标记,1部分退款,2全部退款',
  `equipment_id` bigint(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '设备id',
  `dealer_order_type` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '订单分类(充值订单,消费订单,权益结算订单)',
  `is_wallet_settlement` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否资金池结算',
  `business_scene_merchant_id` bigint(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '经销商商户id',
  `agent_merchant_id` bigint(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '代理商商户id',
  `open_id` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '从member_detail取open_id存储',
  `def6` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL,
  `def7` json DEFAULT NULL,
  `def8` json DEFAULT NULL,
  `def9` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def10` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`, `create_time`),
  KEY `idx_agent_merchant_id` (`agent_merchant_id`),
  KEY `idx_business_scene_merchant_id` (`business_scene_merchant_id`),
  KEY `idx_distributor_id_create_time` (`distributor_id`, `create_time`),
  KEY `idx_open_id` (`open_id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `idx_out_trade_no` (`out_trade_no`, `create_time`),
  KEY `idx_utime` (`update_time`),
  KEY `index_create_time` (`create_time`),
  KEY `index_equipment_id` (`equipment_id`),
  KEY `parent_no` (`parent_no`),
  KEY `user_id` (`user_id`)
)

CREATE TABLE `refund_order_info` (
  `out_trade_no` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL COMMENT '交易订单号',
  `refund_no` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款单号',
  `user_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '用户id',
  `distributor_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '商户id',
  `order_amount` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '订单金额',
  `refund_amount` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款金额',
  `refund_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款详情',
  `status` tinyint(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款状态 1:退款中 2:退款成功 3:退款失败',
  `refund_reason` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL COMMENT '退款原因',
  `refund_time` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP COMMENT '退款发起时间',
  `refund_success_time` timestamp DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款成功时间',
  `refund_type` tinyint(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款类型 1:自动退款 2:手动退款',
  `goods_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款商品详情',
  `id` bigint(11) NOT NULL auto_increment,
  `create_by` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人id',
  `cashier` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作人名称',
  `terminal_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '终端设备id',
  `terminal_name` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '终端设备名称',
  `create_time` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `update_time` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `update_by` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改人',
  `organization_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '组织id',
  `business_type` smallint(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `deduction_amount` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退抵扣金额',
  `store_id` bigint(11) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '店铺id',
  `refund_order_no` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT '退款单号（废弃，用refund_no）',
  `remark` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT '备注',
  `common_extend` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '扩展信息',
  `extend_str` varchar(1024) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务扩展字段',
  `def1` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL,
  `refund_source` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '退款来源',
  `def3` varchar(128) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL,
  `def4` bigint(12) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def11` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def2` tinyint(4) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def13` bigint(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def14` bigint(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def5` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL,
  `def6` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL,
  `marketing_detail` json DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '营销详情',
  `def8` json DEFAULT NULL,
  `def9` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `def10` decimal(11,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`, `refund_time`),
  KEY `btree` (`distributor_id`, `refund_time`),
  KEY `idx_ctime` (`create_time`),
  KEY `idx_out_trade_no` (`out_trade_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `idx_refund_no` (`refund_no`, `refund_time`),
  UNIQUE KEY `idx_refund_order_info_id` (`id`, `refund_time`),
  KEY `idx_utime` (`update_time`),
  KEY `refund_order_time_store_idx` (`refund_time`, `store_id`),
  KEY `user_id` (`user_id`)
)
```

```sql
-- 【order_info】查订单信息
SELECT * FROM order_info where distributor_id='1913197'and user_id ='194947573' and create_time > '2025-11-04 00:00:00'  order by create_time desc;

-- 【refund_order_info】查订单退款信息
select *  from refund_order_info roi where distributor_id ='2347269' and refund_success_time >'2025-11-03 00:00:00' and refund_success_time <'2025-11-04 00:00:00'
```

### 设备

**库：`equipment`**（4 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `device` | 查设备初始化 |
| `iot_equipment` | 查设备信息 |
| `iot_equipment_setting` | 查设备对应的协议 |
| `iot_equipment_docking` | 查安卓设备信息 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE device (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    device_id character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    product_key character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    createdby character varying(32),
    updatedby character varying(32),
    created timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    tenant_id character varying(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT '1131907415637544960'::character varying,
    auth_org_id character varying(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0'::character varying,
    unique_code character varying(32),
    disabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    is_active boolean DEFAULT true,
    transferred boolean DEFAULT false,
    gateway integer DEFAULT 7
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.device_id is '设备唯一标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.product_key is '产品标识符';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.createdby is '创建者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.updatedby is '更新者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.tenant_id is '租户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.auth_org_id is '组织ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.unique_code is '业务唯一编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.disabled is '是否封禁，默认否';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.is_active is '虚拟删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.transferred is '设备是否被转移';
COMMENT ON COLUMN device.gateway is '网关类型 
0：LMQ
1:  NettyGateway
2:  2.0Gateway
3:  ExtendGateway
4:  OpenGateway
5:  BlueTooth
6:  PanguGateway
7:  EMQX';
COMMENT ON TABLE device is '设备表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX device_id_pk ON device USING btree (id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idxi_product_key_device_id_pk ON device USING btree (device_id, product_key);
CREATE INDEX device_unique_code_idx ON device USING btree (unique_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_device_product_key ON device USING btree (product_key);

CREATE TABLE iot_equipment (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    value character varying(40),
    unique_code character varying(32),
    iccid character varying(32),
    imei character varying(64),
    login_flag numeric(10,0),
    factory numeric(12,0),
    mainboard_factory numeric(12,0),
    mainboard_model character varying(255),
    factory_model character varying(255),
    hardware_model character varying(255),
    hardware_project numeric(12,0),
    interface_type character varying(6),
    bluetooth_mac character varying(60),
    mac character varying(60),
    firmware_version character varying(32),
    business_version_no character varying(30),
    initial_time timestamp without time zone,
    factory_type numeric(12,0),
    device_type character varying(10),
    status character varying(12) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    hardware_flag numeric(6,0),
    gateway smallint,
    category integer[],
    firmware_business_no character varying(64),
    firmware_underlay_no character varying(64),
    created timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    is_active boolean,
    createdby numeric(12,0),
    updatedby numeric(12,0),
    private_key character varying(64),
    pkey_password character varying(32),
    bluetooth_key character varying(64)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.unique_code is '设备唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.iccid is 'iccid卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.imei is 'imei码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.login_flag is '登录标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.factory is '生产工厂';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.mainboard_factory is '主板工厂';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.mainboard_model is '主板型号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.factory_model is '生产工厂设备型号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.hardware_model is '硬件网关编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.hardware_project is '硬件产品';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.interface_type is '接口类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.bluetooth_mac is '蓝牙mac地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.mac is 'mac地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.firmware_version is '固件版本号/version_no';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.business_version_no is '业务版本号/business_version_no';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.initial_time is '初始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.factory_type is '出厂设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.device_type is '设备类型 LYY/LYY2';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.status is '设备状态(启用:非disabled字段均可，默认使用1Normal   禁用:disabled)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.hardware_flag is '设备底层标识';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.gateway is '网关';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.category is '设备形态(array格式) / 蓝牙、刷脸、反扫
(1、反扫
2、刷脸
3、蓝牙)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.firmware_business_no is '硬件业务版本/firmware_no';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.firmware_underlay_no is '硬件底层版本';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.is_active is '是否可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.createdby is '创建者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.updatedby is '更新者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.private_key is '设备私钥';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.pkey_password is '私钥密码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment.bluetooth_key is '蓝牙密钥';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX iot_equipment_pkey ON iot_equipment USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX iot_equipment_mac_idx ON iot_equipment USING btree (mac, is_active) WHERE (mac IS NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX iot_equipment_iccid_idx ON iot_equipment USING btree (iccid);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX iot_equipment_unique_code_unqidx ON iot_equipment USING btree (unique_code);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX iot_equipment_value_qunidx ON iot_equipment USING btree (value);
CREATE INDEX iot_equipment_login_flag_idx ON iot_equipment USING btree (login_flag);
CREATE INDEX idx_iot_equipment_fversion ON iot_equipment USING btree (firmware_version);

CREATE TABLE iot_equipment_setting (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_id numeric(12,0),
    product_number character varying(32),
    system_version character varying(50),
    is_pulse character(1),
    app_version character varying(50),
    signal integer,
    pulse_interval smallint,
    interface_baud_rate character varying(6),
    pulse_width_one numeric(12,0),
    pulse_width_two numeric(12,0),
    standby_status character varying(2),
    support_grip character varying(2),
    support_gift character varying(2),
    paper_model character varying(6),
    pulse_duration_ratio smallint,
    gateway_equipment_value character varying(32),
    factory_brand character varying(32),
    created timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    is_active boolean,
    createdby numeric(12,0),
    updatedby numeric(12,0),
    standby_level smallint,
    pulse_width smallint,
    other_standby_level smallint,
    other_pulse_width smallint,
    other_pulse_interval smallint,
    pulse_interval_one numeric,
    pulse_interval_two numeric,
    protocol_id character varying(20),
    gps_support smallint,
    return_ability jsonb,
    low_power boolean
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.product_number is '主板产品编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.system_version is '系统版本号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.is_pulse is '是否开启脉冲间隔 / isinterval';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.app_version is 'app版本号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.signal is '信号值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.pulse_interval is '脉冲间隔';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.interface_baud_rate is '接口波特率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.pulse_duration_ratio is '脉冲转换率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.gateway_equipment_value is '网关设备号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.factory_brand is '工厂品牌';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.is_active is '是否可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.createdby is '创建者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.updatedby is '更新者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.standby_level is '待机电平,0 常开 1常闭';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.pulse_width is '脉冲宽度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.other_standby_level is '额外设备-待机电平 ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.other_pulse_width is '额外设备-脉冲宽度 ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.other_pulse_interval is '额外设置-脉冲间隔';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.pulse_interval_one is '脉冲间隔one';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.pulse_interval_two is '脉冲间隔two';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.protocol_id is '所属协议id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.gps_support is '是否支持GPS: 0-不支持, 1-支持, NULL-未知';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.return_ability is '设备还车能力';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_setting.low_power is '是否低功耗';
COMMENT ON TABLE iot_equipment_setting is '设备设置参数';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX iot_equipment_setting_pkey ON iot_equipment_setting USING btree (id);
CREATE INDEX iot_equipment_setting_equipment_id_idx ON iot_equipment_setting USING hash (equipment_id);

CREATE TABLE iot_equipment_docking (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_id numeric(12,0),
    equipment_value character varying(40),
    user_name character varying(25),
    phone character varying(25),
    screen_width smallint,
    screen_type smallint,
    screen_height smallint,
    resolution_height smallint,
    resolution_with smallint,
    screen_size character varying(64),
    resolution character varying(31),
    rolling_frequency smallint,
    app_id character varying(32),
    app_secret character varying(100),
    app_uuid character varying(64),
    switch_type smallint,
    face_equipment_type character varying(100),
    serial_unique_code character varying(200),
    app_type character varying(20),
    android_unique_code character varying(64),
    third_factory character varying(20),
    created timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp without time zone,
    is_active boolean,
    createdby numeric(12,0),
    updatedby numeric(12,0),
    connect_type smallint,
    connect_mode integer[],
    protocol_version character varying(32),
    mainboard_id numeric(12,0)
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.equipment_id is '设备ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.user_name is '管理员姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.phone is '管理员手机号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.screen_width is '屏幕宽度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.screen_type is '屏幕类型
(1-福袋机屏,2-落地屏)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.screen_height is '屏幕高度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.resolution_height is '屏幕分辨率高度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.resolution_with is '屏幕分辨率宽度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.screen_size is '屏幕大小';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.resolution is '分辨率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.rolling_frequency is '滚动频率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.app_id is '接入应用编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.app_secret is '接入私钥';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.app_uuid is '接入设备uuid';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.switch_type is '接入方式( 0 接口接入 1 盒子接入)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.face_equipment_type is '刷脸设备型号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.serial_unique_code is '设备序列唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.app_type is 'app 类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.android_unique_code is 'android唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.third_factory is '对接第三方工厂';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.is_active is '是否可用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.createdby is '创建者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.updatedby is '更新者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.protocol_version is '协议版本号，登陆时会更新，目前用于sdk设备';
COMMENT ON COLUMN iot_equipment_docking.mainboard_id is '主板id';
COMMENT ON TABLE iot_equipment_docking is '设备对接参数';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX iot_equipment_docking_pkey ON iot_equipment_docking USING btree (id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uidx_appid_uuid ON iot_equipment_docking USING btree (app_id, app_uuid);
CREATE INDEX iot_equipment_docking_equipment_id_idx ON iot_equipment_docking USING hash (equipment_id);
```

```sql
-- 【device】查设备初始化
SELECT * FROM lyy.device WHERE device_id=？;

-- 【iot_equipment】查设备信息
SELECT id, unique_code, iccid, imei, login_flag, factory, mainboard_factory, mainboard_model, factory_model, hardware_model, hardware_project, interface_type, bluetooth_mac, mac, firmware_version, business_version_no, initial_time, factory_type, device_type, status, hardware_flag, gateway, category, firmware_business_no, firmware_underlay_no, private_key, pkey_password, bluetooth_key FROM lyy.iot_equipment WHERE unique_code=？;

-- 【iot_equipment_setting】查设备对应的协议
SELECT id, equipment_id, product_number, system_version, is_pulse, app_version, signal,protocol_id, gps_support, return_ability, low_power FROM lyy.iot_equipment_setting WHERE equipment_id=？;

-- 【iot_equipment_docking】查安卓设备信息
SELECT id, equipment_id, equipment_value, app_id, app_secret, app_uuid, switch_type, face_equipment_type, serial_unique_code, app_type, android_unique_code, third_factory,  connect_type, connect_mode, protocol_version, mainboard_id FROM lyy.iot_equipment_docking WHERE equipment_value=？;
```

**库：`master`**（1 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `lyy_equipment_private_key` | 查设备密钥 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key (
    lyy_equipment_private_key_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    unique_code character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    private_key character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    password character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    ad_org_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    ad_client_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    isactive character(1) NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updatedby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key.lyy_equipment_private_key_id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key.unique_code is '唯一码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key.private_key is '私钥';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key.password is '密码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key.ad_org_id is '废弃';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key is '设备密钥';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_lyy_equipment_private_key ON adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key USING btree (lyy_equipment_private_key_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idxi_equipment_privite_key_uniquecode ON adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key USING btree (unique_code);
```

```sql
-- 【lyy_equipment_private_key】查设备密钥
SELECT lyy_equipment_private_key_id, unique_code, private_key, password FROM adempiere.lyy_equipment_private_key WHERE unique_code=？;
```

### 支付

**库：`master`**（7 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `payment_fee_deduction_equipment` | 查手续费外扣 |
| `experience_merchant_list` | 查体验期 |
| `lyy_merchant_info` | 查商户信息 |
| `lyy_divide_merchant_info` | 查分账用户信息 |
| `lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail` | 查商家资料 |
| `lyy_payment` | 查提现情况 |
| `payment_info_config` | 查appid路由配置 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    distributor_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    distributor_name character varying(32),
    equipment_value character varying(40),
    equipment_type_value character varying(32),
    deduction_merchant_code character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    fee_member_id_code character varying(32),
    fee_member_principal_name character varying(40),
    out_app_id character varying(64),
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    create_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    update_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    create_by bigint,
    update_by bigint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.id is 'id, 主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.distributor_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.distributor_name is '运营商名称,注册时的商户名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.equipment_type_value is '设备类型编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.deduction_merchant_code is '外扣商户号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.fee_member_id_code is '手续费承担方证件id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.fee_member_principal_name is '手续费承担方主体名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.out_app_id is '接入方outAppId';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment.status is '状态 0-不可用 1-可用';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payment_fee_deduction_equipment_pkey ON adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment USING btree (id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payment_fee_deduction_equipment_value_merchant_code_unique_idx ON adempiere.payment_fee_deduction_equipment USING btree (equipment_value, deduction_merchant_code);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.experience_merchant_list (
    experience_merchant_list_id integer NOT NULL,
    ad_org_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    ad_org_value character varying(40),
    app_id character varying(60),
    lyy_swiftpass_merchant_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    submit_entry_time timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    created_by numeric(12,0),
    updated_by numeric(12,0),
    is_active boolean,
    workday smallint,
    open_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    channel_code smallint,
    close_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    effective_time_end timestamp(6) without time zone,
    merchant_name character varying(60),
    merchant_code character varying(60),
    remark character varying(255),
    real_name_show_dialog smallint,
    status smallint,
    expire_notice_day smallint DEFAULT 7,
    support_group_divide boolean,
    ignore_real_name_codes character varying(128),
    support_client_check boolean,
    wechat_client_status smallint,
    alipay_client_status smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.experience_merchant_list_id is 'id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.ad_org_id is '运营商id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.ad_org_value is '运营商账号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.app_id is 'app_id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_id is '商户资料id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.submit_entry_time is '提交进件时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.workday is '工作日';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.open_time is '体验期开启时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.channel_code is '渠道code 4-汇付(已废弃)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.close_time is '体验期关闭时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.effective_time_end is '有效期结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.merchant_name is '商户全称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.merchant_code is '渠道商户号(已废弃)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.real_name_show_dialog is '实名弹窗 0-不弹窗  1-弹窗';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.status is '状态 0 初始 1 有效 2-失效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.expire_notice_day is '过期通知天数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.support_group_divide is '是否支持场地分账（v4.0 新增）。TRUE=走 MerchantGroupDivideRule 新链路；FALSE/NULL=走 ExperienceMerchantDivideRule 旧链路（默认）。NULL 表示从未开启过。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.ignore_real_name_codes is '体验期关闭时忽略实名的渠道编码，多个渠道用英文逗号分隔';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.support_client_check is '是否支持支付端检查体验期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.wechat_client_status is '微信端是否在体验期 0=否 1=是';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.experience_merchant_list.alipay_client_status is '支付宝端是否在体验期 0=否 1=是';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.experience_merchant_list is '体验期商户清单';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX experience_merchant_list_pkey ON adempiere.experience_merchant_list USING btree (experience_merchant_list_id);
CREATE INDEX experience_merchant_list_ad_org_id_idx ON adempiere.experience_merchant_list USING btree (ad_org_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX experience_merchant_list_ad_org_id_swiftpass_merchant_id_idx ON adempiere.experience_merchant_list USING btree (ad_org_id, lyy_swiftpass_merchant_id);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.lyy_merchant_info (
    lyy_merchant_info_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    merchant_code character varying(255) NOT NULL,
    ad_org_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    channel_code smallint NOT NULL,
    goods_type_code character varying(32),
    open_pay_type jsonb,
    fee_rate double precision NOT NULL,
    status smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone,
    remark character varying(255),
    support_client_type jsonb,
    divide_type smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    settlement_type smallint DEFAULT 0,
    real_name_status smallint DEFAULT 0,
    lyy_swiftpass_merchant_id numeric(10,0),
    alipay_real_name_status smallint DEFAULT 0,
    settlement_period smallint,
    channel_entry_type smallint,
    common_extend jsonb
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.lyy_merchant_info_id is '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.merchant_code is '进件后颁发的商户code';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.ad_org_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.channel_code is '渠道code 1:威富通;2:富友;3:网商;4:云闪付';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.goods_type_code is '商品类型code，兼容有设备的和无设备的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.open_pay_type is '开通的支付类型 0:未开通，1:已开通无子商户号，其他值:已开通该值为商户号，微信(WechatPay)、支付宝(AliPay)、小程序(WechatMiniPay)、云闪付(UnionPay)子商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.fee_rate is '进件费率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.status is '商户状态枚举 1:正常;2:冻结(可解冻使用);3:禁用(永久不可用)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.updated is '修改时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.support_client_type is '支持的客户端类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.divide_type is '分账属性类型,0:无分账权限 1:有分账权限 2:有但暂停分账权限';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.settlement_type is '1正常结算 2延迟结算';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.real_name_status is '是否已实名认证 0:未实名 1已实名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.alipay_real_name_status is '支付宝是否已实名认证 0:未实名 1已实名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.settlement_period is '结算周期,默认D+1 0:手动 1:D+1';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.channel_entry_type is '渠道进件类型 1=商户进件 2=用户进件';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_merchant_info.common_extend is '扩展字段';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.lyy_merchant_info is '进件后的商户表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX lyy_merchant_info_pkey ON adempiere.lyy_merchant_info USING btree (lyy_merchant_info_id);

COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.inner_img is '内景照';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.alipay_logon_id is '商户实名支付宝账号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_name is '联系人姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_phone is '联系人手机';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_id_code is '联系人身份证号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_id_code_expire is '联系人身份证有效期,例如2020-01-01,2030-01-01';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_id_card_face_img is '联系人身份证人面像照（反面）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_id_card_national_img is '联系人身份证人面像照（反面）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.cert_type is '登记证书类型，1事业单位法人证书 2统一社会信用代码证书 3有偿服务许可证（军队医院适用） 4医疗机构执业许可证（军队医院适用） 5企业营业执照（挂靠企业的党组织适用） 6组织机构代码证（政府机关适用） 7社会团体法人登记证书 8民办非企业单位登记证书 9基金会法人登记证书 10慈善组织公开募捐资格证书 11农民专业合作社法人营业执照 12宗教活动场所登记证 13其他证书/批文/证明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.company_cert_img is '单位证明函';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.industry_type_code is '商户所属行业机构类型CODE';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.industry_qualification_img is '商户所属行业机构特殊资质图片,可多张用,分割';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.identity_address is '身份证地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.business_authorization_letter_img is '业务办理授权函';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.business_authorization_letter_alipay_img is '支付宝业务办理授权函';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.company_cert_alipay_img is '支付宝单位证明函';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.payable_sms_flag is '支付开通短信提醒标识，二进制下标0：未发送 1：已发送，例如数值23转换二进制10111表示：京东已发送，支付宝未发送，微信已发送，微信小程序已发送，云闪付已发送';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.id_code_expire_end is '法人身份有效结束日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.license_expire_end is '营业执照有效期结束日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.contact_id_code_expire_end is '联系人身份证有效期接收日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail.business_category is '经营类目,微信支付宝实名用到,1:门店场所 2:流动经营/便民服务 3:线上商品/服';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail_pkey ON adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail USING btree (lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail_id);
CREATE INDEX lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail_org_idx ON adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail USING btree (ad_org_id);
CREATE INDEX lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail_parent_id_idx ON adempiere.lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail USING btree (lyy_swiftpass_merchant_id);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.lyy_payment (
    lyy_payment_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    payto_org_id numeric(10,0),
    documentno character varying(30),
    docaction character varying(2),
    c_doctype_id character varying(10),
    processed character(1),
    docstatus character varying(30),
    status character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    description character varying(255),
    ad_org_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    ad_client_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    isactive character(1) NOT NULL,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    createdby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updatedby numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    payment_date timestamp without time zone,
    amount numeric(8,2) NOT NULL,
    payment_type character(1) DEFAULT NULL::bpchar,
    wx_billno character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    service_fee numeric,
    actual_pay numeric,
    bank_name character varying(60) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    type character(1) DEFAULT '1'::bpchar,
    source_type smallint,
    pay_channel_id numeric(10,0),
    receive_account character varying(64),
    receive_name character varying(64),
    remark character varying(255),
    transfer_remark character varying(64),
    channel_code smallint,
    merchant_code character varying(255),
    receive_org_id numeric(11,0),
    receive_org_type character varying(10),
    settle_no character varying(64),
    receive_identity_type character varying(36),
    transfer_account character varying(64),
    check_query_count smallint,
    wallet_id numeric(12,0) DEFAULT NULL::numeric,
    wallet_account_name character varying(64) DEFAULT NULL::character varying,
    wallet_uid bigint,
    receive_card_id bigint,
    withdraw_method smallint,
    batch_no character varying(64),
    common_extend jsonb,
    transfer_settlement_type character varying(32),
    funding_disburse_type smallint,
    payment_no character varying(32),
    withdraw_payment_no character varying(32),
    FOREIGN KEY (payto_org_id) REFERENCES ad_org(ad_org_id) ON UPDATE RESTRICT ON DELETE RESTRICT
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.lyy_payment_id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.payto_org_id is '提现商家';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.documentno is '提现单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.docaction is '废弃';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.c_doctype_id is '废弃';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.processed is '废弃';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.docstatus is '废弃';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.status is 'Apply-初始 Finished-成功 Refuse-已拒绝';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.description is '描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.ad_org_id is '废弃';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.payment_date is '提现成功时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.amount is '提现金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.payment_type is 'A-支付宝 B-银行卡 W-微信';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.wx_billno is '微信单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.service_fee is '手续费';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.actual_pay is '实际到账金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.bank_name is '银行名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.type is '1:支付收益 2:广告收益 3:电子秤的收益 4:二清支付收益';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.source_type is '申请单来源类型,空或1:商户申请 2:签署协议后自动生成 3:人工导出 4:人工导入';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.pay_channel_id is '打款渠道id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.receive_account is '收款人账号,可以是openId 支付宝账号  银行卡号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.receive_name is '收款人姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.remark is '说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.transfer_remark is '转账备注,送往微信或支付宝或银联';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.channel_code is '渠道编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.merchant_code is '渠道商户号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.receive_org_id is '收款人商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.receive_org_type is '收款人类型 空默认主商户,DIV:分成方';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.settle_no is '结算单号 type=4时有值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.receive_identity_type is '接收方身份类型 支付宝的会员ID=ALIPAY_USER_ID，支付宝登录号=ALIPAY_LOGON_ID 支付宝openid=ALIPAY_OPEN_ID 微信openId=WECHAT_OPEN_ID 银行卡=BANK_CARD_NO';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.transfer_account is '付款账号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.check_query_count is '回查次数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.wallet_id is '钱包id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.wallet_account_name is '钱包名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.wallet_uid is '钱包用户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.receive_card_id is '收款人资料id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.withdraw_method is '1-自动提现 2-手动提现  默认1';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.batch_no is '提现批次号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.common_extend is '通用扩展信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.transfer_settlement_type is '付款类型：FLEXIBLE_EMPLOYMENT-灵活用工 / WECHAT_TO_BALANCE-微信转账到余额 / ALIPAY_TO_BALANCE-支付宝转账到余额 / ALIPAY_TO_BANKCARD-支付宝转账到银行卡 / GLOBAL_FUND_PAYMENT-全域资金付款 / BALANCE_PAY-余额支付 / DM_WITHDRAW-dm取现';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.funding_disburse_type is '资金拨付类型 1-仅付款 2-仅提现 3-付款+提现';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.payment_no is '关联付款单号（类型1/3 时指向 clearing_payment_record.payment_no，类型2 时为 null）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.lyy_payment.withdraw_payment_no is '提现单号（WD 前缀，类型2/3 时有值，类型1 时为 null）';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.lyy_payment is '提现记录表';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pk_lyy_payment ON adempiere.lyy_payment USING btree (lyy_payment_id);
CREATE INDEX lyy_payment_payto_org_status_idx ON adempiere.lyy_payment USING btree (payto_org_id, status);
CREATE INDEX lyy_payment_wx_billno_idx ON adempiere.lyy_payment USING btree (wx_billno);
CREATE INDEX lyy_payment_idx_settle_no ON adempiere.lyy_payment USING btree (settle_no);
CREATE INDEX lyy_payment_idx_created ON adempiere.lyy_payment USING btree (created);
CREATE INDEX lyy_payment_idx_batch_no ON adempiere.lyy_payment USING btree (batch_no);

CREATE TABLE adempiere.payment_info_config (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    distributor_id bigint NOT NULL,
    channel_code smallint NOT NULL,
    merchant_code character varying(255) NOT NULL,
    config_value character varying(255) NOT NULL,
    config_type smallint NOT NULL,
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    card_id bigint,
    out_app_id character varying(64),
    extend_info jsonb,
    created timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp without time zone NOT NULL,
    created_by bigint,
    updated_by bigint,
    deleted boolean DEFAULT false
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.distributor_id is '商户id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.channel_code is '渠道编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.merchant_code is '渠道商户号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.config_value is '配置的值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.config_type is '配置类型 1=appid配置 2=支付目录配置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.status is '状态 0=初始 1=处理中 2=成功 3=失败 4=无效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.card_id is '商户资料id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.out_app_id is '接入方系统id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.extend_info is '扩展信息，json 格式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.updated is '修改时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.created_by is '创建人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.updated_by is '修改人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN adempiere.payment_info_config.deleted is '逻辑删除标识 false=有效 true=删除';
COMMENT ON TABLE adempiere.payment_info_config is '支付信息配置表';
CREATE INDEX payment_info_config_idx ON adempiere.payment_info_config USING btree (channel_code, config_value, config_type);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payment_info_config_pkey ON adempiere.payment_info_config USING btree (id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payment_info_config_unique_idx ON adempiere.payment_info_config USING btree (distributor_id, channel_code, merchant_code, config_value, config_type);
```

```sql
-- 【payment_fee_deduction_equipment】查手续费外扣
select *
from payment_fee_deduction_equipment
where equipment_value ='12200001';

-- 【experience_merchant_list】查体验期
select *
from experience_merchant_list
where ad_org_id = '1094750';

-- 【lyy_merchant_info】查商户信息
select *
from lyy_merchant_info
where ad_org_id = 1094949;

-- 【lyy_divide_merchant_info】查分账用户信息
select *
from lyy_divide_merchant_info
where  ad_org_id=1094552;

-- 【lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail】查商家资料
select * from lyy_swiftpass_merchant_detail where ad_org_id = 1073610;

-- 【lyy_payment】查提现情况
select *
from lyy_payment
where ad_org_id in (1036475)
order by created desc;

-- 【payment_info_config】查appid路由配置
select * from payment_info_config where distributor_id = 2267406
```

### 按摩

**库：`advert`**（4 张表）

| 表名 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- |
| `insurance_user_benefit_card` | 权益表 |
| `insurance_order` | 权益订单表 |
| `automatic_debit_order` | 代扣订单 |
| `automatic_debit_receive_record` | 代扣记录 |

```sql
CREATE TABLE insurance_user_benefit_card (
    id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    card_sku_id character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    card_name character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    config_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    distributor_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    user_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    card_code character varying(30) NOT NULL,
    card_pass character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    out_trade_no character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    price numeric(9,2) NOT NULL,
    equipment_type character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    equipment_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_group_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    equipment_group_name character varying(32) NOT NULL,
    open_id character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    order_source smallint NOT NULL,
    order_channel smallint NOT NULL,
    deleted smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    subsidies_amount numeric(10,2),
    third_reward_type smallint,
    piao_piao_type character varying(24),
    commodity_id numeric(11,0),
    use_status smallint,
    use_phone character varying(12),
    remark character varying(128),
    parent_id numeric(11,0),
    product_type smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.card_sku_id is '权益卡商品ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.card_name is '权益卡名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.config_id is '配置Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.distributor_id is '商家ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.status is '状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.card_code is '卡码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.card_pass is '卡密';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.out_trade_no is '订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.created is '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.updated is '更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.price is '权益卡金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.equipment_type is '设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.equipment_id is '设备Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.equipment_group_id is '设备场地Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.equipment_group_name is '设备场地名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.open_id is 'open_id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.order_source is '订单来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.order_channel is '订单渠道';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.deleted is '是否删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.subsidies_amount is '补贴金额（元）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.third_reward_type is '权益来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.piao_piao_type is '权益类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.commodity_id is '商品ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.use_status is '使用状态 领取状态 0、未领取 1、领取中 2、已领取';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.use_phone is '使用手机号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.remark is '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.parent_id is '关联父ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_user_benefit_card.product_type is '产品类型用于区分商创业务1=保障 2=权益卡 3=附带商品';
COMMENT ON TABLE insurance_user_benefit_card is '权益用户卡记录表';
CREATE INDEX idx_iubc_parent_id ON insurance_user_benefit_card USING btree (parent_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_insurance_user_benefit_card_created ON insurance_user_benefit_card USING btree (created);
CREATE INDEX idx_insurance_user_benefit_card_osetp ON insurance_user_benefit_card USING btree (order_source, equipment_type, product_type);

CREATE TABLE insurance_order (
    id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    order_time timestamp(6) without time zone DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    end_time timestamp(6) without time zone DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    out_trade_no character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    insurance_no character varying(64),
    equipment_type character varying(8) NOT NULL,
    equipment_value character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    equipment_id numeric(11,0),
    lyy_user_id numeric(11,0),
    distributor_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_group_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_group_name character varying(255),
    order_source smallint,
    open_id character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    user_name character varying(64),
    router_id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,
    insurance_name character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    insurance_price numeric(5,2) NOT NULL,
    order_channel smallint,
    equip_status smallint NOT NULL,
    order_status smallint NOT NULL,
    insurance_status smallint NOT NULL,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    click_status smallint,
    vertical smallint,
    provider character varying(20),
    benefit_name character varying(20),
    config_id numeric(11,0),
    sku_id character varying(64),
    brand character varying(20)
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (created);
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.id is '主键id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.order_time is '下单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.end_time is '结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.out_trade_no is '订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.insurance_no is '保单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.equipment_type is '设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.equipment_value is '设备编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.equipment_id is '设备Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.lyy_user_id is '用户Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.distributor_id is '商家Id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.equipment_group_id is '设备场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.equipment_group_name is '设备场地名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.order_source is '订单来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.open_id is '用户openId';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.user_name is '用户名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN insurance_order.router_id is '路由Id';

CREATE TABLE automatic_debit_order (
    id numeric(11,0) NOT NULL,

    distributor_id numeric(11,0),
    group_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_id numeric(11,0),

    equipment_type numeric(11,0),
    client character varying(16),
    channel_id character varying(64),
    debit_type smallint,
    phone_number character varying(32),
    first_pay_amount character varying(32),
    debit_amount character varying(32),
    execute_time character varying(32),
    period character varying(32),
    status smallint,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    createdby bigint,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    updatedby bigint,
    channel_order_no character varying(64),
    extra_info jsonb,
    business_order_no character varying(64),

    platform character varying(32),

    first_debit_period character varying(20)
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.distributor_id is '商家id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.group_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.equipment_id is '设备id';

COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.channel_id is '渠道id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.debit_type is '扣费方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.phone_number is '手机号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.first_pay_amount is '首笔金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.debit_amount is '扣费金额';

COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.period is '执行时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.status is '0-未支付,1-支付完成,2-取消,3-签约成功，-1失败';

COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.channel_code is '渠道编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN automatic_debit_order.unsign_time is '解约时间';
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE massage_automatic_debit (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL,
    order_no character varying(64),
    parent_id numeric(12,0),
    user_id numeric(11,0),
    wechat_user_id numeric(11,0),
    merchant_id numeric(11,0),
    store_id numeric(11,0),
    equipment_value character varying(32),
    equipment_type numeric(11,0),
    product_type smallint,
    phone_number character varying(32),
    first_pay_amount numeric(10,2),
    first_debit_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    debit_amount numeric(10,2),
    debit_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    start_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    end_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    cancel_time timestamp(6) without time zone,
    status smallint,
    third_order_no character varying(64),
    promotion_activity_id numeric(11,0),
    order_source smallint,
    order_position smallint,
    reward_config jsonb,
    extend_info jsonb,
    deleted boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    created timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    createdby bigint,
    updated timestamp(6) without time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone,
    updatedby bigint,
    sign_channel character varying(20),
    client character varying(20),
    sign_user_id numeric(11,0),
    refund_status smallint
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.order_no is '乐摇摇订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.parent_id is '父扣费流水ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.user_id is '乐摇摇支付宝用户id';

COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.store_id is '场地id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.equipment_value is '设备编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.equipment_type is '设备类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.product_type is '产品类型：1=超级VIP ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.phone_number is '手机号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.first_pay_amount is '首笔金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.first_debit_time is '首次签约时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.debit_amount is '扣费金额';

COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.start_time is '开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.end_time is '到期时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.cancel_time is '解约时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.status is '0=未支付(签约未扣款) 1-支付成功(扣款成功) 2-支付失败(扣款失败)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.third_order_no is '渠道订单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.promotion_activity_id is '活动ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.order_source is '订单来源 0：微信 1：支付宝';

COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.sign_channel is '签约渠道';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.client is '签约用户权益客户端';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.sign_user_id is '签约用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN massage_automatic_debit.refund_status is '退款状态 0或者空=未退款 1=退款中 2=已退款 3=退款失败';
 
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX massage_automatic_debit_pkey ON massage_automatic_debit USING btree (id);

CREATE TABLE jd_insurance_start_record (
    id numeric(12,0) NOT NULL DEFAULT nextval('jd_insurance_start_record_seq'::regclass),

    order_no character varying(32),
    reward_type smallint DEFAULT 1,
    extend_info jsonb
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN jd_insurance_start_record.update_time is '更新时间';

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX jd_insurance_start_record_pkey ON jd_insurance_start_record USING btree (id);

CREATE TABLE promotion_activity_user (
    promotion_activity_user_id numeric(10,0) NOT NULL,
    promotion_activity_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_user_id numeric(12,0),
    lyy_distributor_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_equipment_group_id numeric(10,0),
    lyy_equipment_type_id numeric(10,0),
    reward_type smallint NOT NULL,
    reward_details jsonb,
    end_time timestamp(3) without time zone,
    status smallint NOT NULL,
    winning smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    updated timestamp(3) without time zone NOT NULL,
    start_time timestamp(3) without time zone,
    equipment_id numeric(10,0),
    receive_time timestamp(6) without time zone
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (created);
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.promotion_activity_user_id is '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.promotion_activity_id is '活动ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.lyy_user_id is '用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.lyy_distributor_id is '商户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.lyy_equipment_group_id is '场地ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.lyy_equipment_type_id is '设备类型ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.reward_type is '
0, 充值满赠支付立减券（随机金额）
1, 充值满赠支付立减券（固定金额）
2, 充值满赠充值送币券（随机币数）
3, 充值满赠充值送币券（固定币数）
4, 随机金额立减
5, 固定金额立减
6, 满赠送币机会（随机币）
7, 满赠送币机会（固定币）
8, 限时优惠（现有场地套餐打折）
9, 限时优惠（自定义限时套餐)
10, 新用户特惠（首单打折）
11, 新用户特惠（首单扣减固定金额）
12, 通行证激励金（平台）
13, 乐摇摇平台券
14, 摇奖免单券(随机立减)
15, 免单券 
16, 赠送券 
17, 体验券 
18, 广告红包,
19, 限时折扣活动配置
20,礼品兑换券
21,体验折扣券
22,充值满赠支付折扣券
23,新用户特惠(自定义新用户专享套餐)
24,广告打折活动
25,平台随机立减活动(金额)
26,平台随机立减活动(比例)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.reward_details is '奖品属性 json串';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.end_time is '过期时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.status is '
(1, "待支付"),
(2, "待使用"),
(3, "已使用"),
(4, "已过期");';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.winning is '抽奖状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.created is '记录创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.updated is '记录更新时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.start_time is '开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.equipment_id is '记录对应设备id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN promotion_activity_user.receive_time is '领取时间';
COMMENT ON TABLE promotion_activity_user is '用户活动领取记录表';
```

| `selectdb_payment` | 5 | `dwd_payment_payment_record_trade`、`dwd_payment_refund_record_trade`、`ads_order_info_agg`、`ods_unified_redeem_order`、`ads_payment_equipment_record_di` |
| `merchant` | 1 | `merchant_equipment_board` |
| `middle_stage` | 1 | `ticket` |
| `lyy_infor` | 5 | `xiaole_user_points_record`、`xiaole_life_user`、`xiaole_life_user_relate`、`xiaole_black_user`、`xiaole_user_no_app_request` |
| `order` | 2 | `order_info`、`refund_order_info` |
| `equipment` | 4 | `device`、`iot_equipment`、`iot_equipment_setting`、`iot_equipment_docking` |
| `advert` | 4 | `insurance_user_benefit_card`、`insurance_order`、`automatic_debit_order`、`automatic_debit_receive_record` |

## 四、数据一致性校验

表名列与示例 SQL 中 `FROM`/`JOIN` 的表名不一致的记录：

| Excel 行 | 业务分类 | 库 | 表名列 | SQL 中的表 | 使用场景 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 56 | 洗水 | `cmember` | `promotion_activity_merchant` | `wash_activity_rule_equipment` | 查洗水商家维度商创活动 |

> 注：Excel 第 52 行的 `ds_unified_redeem_order` 为录入手误，实际表名是 `ods_unified_redeem_order`（抖音、美团核销数据同表，按 `channel` 字段区分），已按正确表名统一。
